Abstract: Audits of generative AI (GenAI) systems often summarize behavior as a reported rate: how often the audited system complies with policy. Researchers and stakeholders use that rate to compare systems, track regressions, and gate deployment. A reported rate reflects both the system under audit and the measurement choices behind it, so a change in the rate can leave it unclear whether the system or those choices moved. We introduce ASSERT, a specification-driven measurement pipeline for GenAI audits that ties each reported rate to a written specification of the measurement choices used to produce it. ASSERT helps draft a behavioral rubric and test cases, then runs the audit against a GenAI system and returns a reported rate. In a case study on conversational deception, we observe that the reported rate moves substantially with the dialogue setup, the simulated user, the judge, and the evidence bar for non-compliance. These measurement choices substantially change the reported rate and can reorder GenAI system rankings. Because each reported rate is tied to an explicit specification, differences across audits are easier to attribute and interpret.

</details>


### [84] [Bootstrapping Niche Multilingual Code Translation via Reinforcement Learning with Execution-Based Verifiable Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13854)
*Kouki Yuki,Jie Zeng,Kyoko Ogawa,Ryunosuke Ikeda,Yohei Kobashi,Takeshi Kojima,Ikuya Yamada,Yusuke Iwasawa,Yutaka Matsuo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于执行监督的偏好强化学习方法来提升多语言（many-to-many）代码翻译质量。通过将可验证的Python种子程序扩展为多语言执行验证代码池，训练奖励模型来评估跨语言翻译质量，并使用GRPO对25种语言的600个语言对进行优化。引入HumanEval-X++基准，在Qwen-3.5 4B和9B模型上取得一致提升，4B模型平均提升13%，中端语言提升21%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经代码翻译主要集中于少数流行语言（如C++、Java、Python），而实际中存在着多对多的翻译需求，在这些语言组合中平行监督数据稀疏，导致模型生成的翻译虽然看似合理但不可执行。作者旨在解决这一被忽视的、监督稀缺的多语言翻译场景。

Method: 1) 数据生成：将可验证的种子Python程序扩展为多语言可执行验证代码池；2) 偏好标注：基座LLM生成跨语言翻译候选，根据执行结果标注偏好；3) 奖励模型：训练奖励模型打分跨语言翻译质量；4) 策略优化：使用GRPO对25×24共600个有向语言对进行优化；5) 基准构建：提出HumanEval-X++执行基准扩展多语言评测空间。

Result: 在HumanEval-X++及现有基准上，Qwen-3.5 4B和9B模型均优于未训练基线。4B模型在HumanEval-X++上平均提升13%，在中端语言上提升21%。

Conclusion: 本文建立了从数据生成、训练到评测的可靠多语言代码翻译流程，验证了基于执行监督的偏好强化学习在多对多代码翻译场景的有效性，为后续进一步自举提升多语言翻译质量奠定了基础。

Abstract: Code translation must preserve executable behavior across many programming languages, yet neural code translation has largely focused on a few popular languages such as C++, Java, and Python. This leaves a niche, many-to-many setting where parallel supervision is sparse, producing plausible but non-executable translations. We address this setting with preference-based reinforcement learning driven by execution-based supervision. Our pipeline firstly expands verifiable seed Python programs into a multilingual pool of execution-validated codes. Using the pool, a base LLM generates translation candidates across language pairs, which we label by their execution outcomes. The resulting preferences are used to train a reward model that scores cross-language translation quality. Finally, we optimize our base LLMs with GRPO over 600 directed language pairs (25 x 24) using the reward model as a signal. To evaluate the niche translation capability, we introduce HumanEval-X++, an execution-based benchmark that extends HumanEval-X to a broad many-to-many language space. We evaluate our approach using Qwen-3.5 4B and 9B models. On HumanEval-X++ and existing benchmarks, it yields consistent gains over the untrained baselines. In particular, the 4B model achieves an average improvement of 13% across all languages on HumanEval-X++, with a gain of 21% on mid-tier languages. Our study establishes a reliable approach of data generation, training, and benchmarking, paving the way toward further bootstrapping the quality of many-to-many translation for programming languages.

</details>


### [85] [Scaling Creative Writing Beyond Story-Centric Data with Attribute-Guided Genre Expansion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13947)
*Hwan Chang,Yongil Kim,Heuiyeen Yeen,Yireun Kim,Jinsik Lee,Hwanhee Lee*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种属性引导的体裁扩展框架，用于将大语言模型的创意写作数据从以故事为中心扩展到涵盖13种创意体裁的50K语料库（Multi-Genre Collection），实验证明该框架训练的模型在创意写作能力上显著优于基线和现有写作语料训练的模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有高质量创意写作数据主要集中于故事类数据，限制了模型遵循多种创意格式的结构性和功能性规范的能力。需要一种能够规模化扩展多样创意体裁写作数据的通用方法。

Method: 该框架将主题广度与体裁形式控制相分离：利用人工创作的故事提示作为多样化的创意种子，结合人工策展的体裁属性来强制实现不同的结构、风格和格式约定，继而提示强LLM生成符合体裁的查询-响应对，最后经过质量过滤。据此构建了Multi-Genre Collection，包含50K样本、覆盖13种创意体裁。

Result: 在分布外写作基准和保留体裁诊断上的实验表明，基于该数据微调的模型在创意写作能力上持续超越基础模型、写作特化基线以及现有写作语料训练的模型。体裁数量消融实验进一步表明，受控的体裁扩展（而非仅扩展故事数据）是提升鲁棒创意写作能力的关键驱动力。

Conclusion: 受控的体裁扩展策略比单纯的故事中心规模扩展更能有效提升LLM的创意写作能力，为构建多样化的高质量创意写作训练数据提供了可行框架。

Abstract: High-quality creative writing data for large language models (LLMs) remains dominated by story-centric data, limiting models' ability to follow the structural and functional conventions of diverse creative formats. We propose an attribute-guided genre expansion framework for scaling creative writing data beyond story generation. By separating thematic breadth from genre-form control, our framework leverages human-authored story prompts as diverse creative seeds, while utilizing manually curated genre attributes to enforce distinct structural, stylistic, and formatting conventions. We combine these to prompt strong LLMs for genre-faithful query-response pairs, which are then quality-filtered. Applying this framework, we construct the Multi-Genre Collection, a 50K-example corpus spanning 13 creative genres, including story, rap, lyrics, scripts, game design, character design, and other creative formats. Experiments across out-of-distribution writing benchmarks and held-out genre diagnostics demonstrate that models fine-tuned on our data consistently surpass not only base models and writing-specialized baselines, but also models trained on existing writing corpora. Genre-count ablations further indicate that controlled genre expansion, rather than story-centric scaling alone, is a key driver of robust creative writing capability.

</details>


### [86] [Repair, Not Improvement: Decomposing Constrained Decoding in Tool-Call Abstention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13959)
*Janghoon Lee*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文测量了约束生成中被先前工作明确搁置的场景——函数调用中的工具弃用（tool abstention）。通过仅修改8个对比条件，分离了文法在"确定生成停止位置"与"限制可发射token"两个职责，在0.6B到4B的开源模型上、匹配的英语/韩语项目上进行了评估。结果显示，在六个单元中四个单元上，先前工作的对比在工具弃用上的效应为负（最差-29.5点），没有任何单元呈现显著正效应。作者发现约束恢复的是"形式"而非"正确性"：修复的698个弃用案例中545个原本无可读答案，0个是被评分者拒绝的判断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 约束生成（constrained generation）的先前研究明确将函数调用排除在其分析之外：其声称解码器对格式约束的贡献很小，但在第7节警告不可将结论外推到约束编码正确性要求的情形，并将函数调用列为这种情形。工具弃用正是这种情形最尖锐的体现：一个枚举类型本身不改变答案措辞，只是缩小答案集合，而"不调用任何工具"是首先被排除的选项。作者旨在测量这个被先前工作排除的情形。

Method: 实验设计为三个条件作用于同一字节级相同的提示，以分离文法的两个职责：一是固定生成停止的位置（stop token），二是限制可发射的token（enum）。作者在0.6B至4B的开源权重模型上，使用匹配的英语和韩语项目进行评估，实现语言对在项目内部可比。采用预注册的度量指标（工具弃用率）作为主要衡量标准。

Result: 与无约束解码器相比，先前工作中的对比方法在六个单元中四个单元上呈现负效应，且置信区间排除零，最严重的达到-29.5个百分点的性能下降；没有任一单元呈现排除零的正效应。总和数据呈现异号叠加：在最小的韩语模型中，stop token贡献-20.0，enum贡献+19.5，两者合计-0.5。被修复的698个弃用案例中，545个原本没有可读答案，0个是评分者拒绝的判断，说明恢复的主要是输出形式而非内容正确性。在工具必需的项目上效应全部为正；作者指出将汇总数字作为更友好指标反而更不利。两个预注册的语言假设均未得到验证。

Conclusion: 约束生成在函数调用场景（特别是工具弃用）中的表现远不如格式约束场景乐观：文法约束对"正确性要求"的编码贡献可能为负或接近零。约束主要修复的是输出形式（使输出可读）而非判断正确性。作者还指出，选择不同的聚合指标会改变对干预的评价方向，这提示评估方式本身的敏感性。语言间的差异假设未被数据支持。

Abstract: Function calling is what the recent accounting of constrained generation explicitly sets aside: it finds the decoder's contribution small for format constraints, then warns in its Section 7 against extrapolating where a constraint encodes a correctness requirement, and names function calling as one. Tool abstention is that case at its sharpest: an enum leaves the wording of an answer alone and narrows the set of answers there are, and declining to call anything is the first it drops. We measure the excluded case. Three conditions over one byte-identical prompt separate a grammar's two jobs: it fixes where generation stops as well as which tokens may be emitted. We evaluate open-weight models from 0.6B to 4B on matched English and Korean items, so the language comparison is made within item. Against an unconstrained decoder, prior work's contrast is negative on abstention in four of six cells with intervals excluding zero, worst -29.5 points, and positive with an interval excluding zero in none. The total is a sum with opposite signs: on the smallest model in Korean the stop token costs -20.0, the enum returns +19.5, and the two leave -0.5. What it recovers is form: of 698 abstentions repaired, 545 had no readable answer and 0 were judgements the scorer refused. On tool-needed items it is positive throughout; abstention leads because it is the preregistered measure, and the pooled number being kinder to the intervention makes moving to it worse rather than better. Both preregistered language claims fail.

</details>


### [87] [Batch-wise Adaptive Pruning: Periodic Neuron Activation-Aware Weight Pruning for Language Reasoning Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14003)
*Yongmin Kim,Shota Takashiro,Yusuke Iwasawa,Takeshi Kojima,Yutaka Matsuo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对大型推理模型（LRMs）在批处理推理场景下，现有无训练自适应剪枝方法因阈值失配导致稀疏度漂移和精度崩溃的问题，提出了一种结合周期化top-k选择与激活记忆的训练免费自适应剪枝方法，在批大小为4、50%目标稀疏度下，相比SOTA方法在DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-7B上平均准确率提升39.7个百分点，并实现1.40倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LRMs通过扩展思维链在复杂任务上表现优异，但推理成本高昂；生产环境中批处理推理不可或缺，然而现有训练免费自适应剪枝方法在批处理场景下严重退化。原因是批处理必须共享单个剪枝掩码，需将激活跨样本聚合后做阈值选择，但离线校准的阈值与聚合后的分布不匹配，导致实际稀疏度漂移、推理任务准确率崩溃。

Method: 提出两种核心组件：(1) 用周期性top-k选择替代阈值选择，作用于聚合重要性分数，不受聚合引起的激活分布偏移影响，且每个更新周期只执行一次选择而非每个token都执行，保持加速效果；(2) 基于"重要神经元在长推理生成中周期性重新激发"的观察，引入激活记忆机制，跨更新阶段累积重要性，使周期性复现的神经元得以保留。

Result: 在多样化的推理基准上，批大小4、50%目标稀疏度、DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-7B模型下，相比之前最先进的自适应剪枝方法，平均准确率提升39.7个百分点；在50%实际稀疏度下，相对稠密推理达到1.40倍加速。

Conclusion: 该方法证明了通过周期性top-k选择与激活记忆机制，可以在批处理推理场景下有效兼顾LRMs的稀疏化压缩与推理精度，解决了训练免费自适应剪枝方法在批处理中的分布偏移与稀疏度漂移问题。

Abstract: Large Reasoning Models (LRMs) achieve strong performance on complex tasks through extended chain-of-thought generation, but incur substantial computational costs during inference. In production settings, batched inference is essential for high throughput, yet the existing training-free adaptive pruning methods we evaluate severely degrade in this regime. Because a batch must share a single pruning mask, these methods aggregate activations across samples and then apply threshold-based selection; the threshold, calibrated offline on unaggregated activations, no longer matches the aggregated distribution, so the realized sparsity ratio drifts and accuracy on reasoning tasks collapses under batched inference.
  In this work, we propose a training-free adaptive pruning method designed specifically for batched inference in LRMs, built on two components. First, we replace threshold-based selection with periodic top-k selection over the aggregated importance scores, which is unaffected by the shift that aggregation induces in the activation distribution, and which runs selection once per update period rather than at every token, preserving the speedup. Second, based on the observation that important neurons re-fire periodically during long reasoning generation, we introduce an activation memory that accumulates importance across update phases so that recurring neurons are retained.
  Experiments on diverse reasoning benchmarks demonstrate that our method outperforms the previous state-of-the-art adaptive pruning method by 39.7 percentage points in average accuracy at batch size 4 with 50% target sparsity on DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-7B, and reaches 1.40x speedup over dense inference at 50% actual sparsity.

</details>


### [88] [S2Dialog: Multimodal Dialogue Retrieval with Semantic and Acoustic-Style Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14029)
*Xueqi Wang,Zhigang Wang,Runqing Zhang,Zhenqi Jia,Junfeng Zhao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出S2Dialog，一个用于多模态对话库中对话级语义-风格检索的统一框架，通过对话级文本检索器、声学检索器以及文本-声学对比学习实现对话级别的检索，在DailyTalk数据集上取得了优异性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有检索方法局限于话语级或单模态匹配，无法捕捉整个对话的全局语义连贯性和风格一致性，难以满足对话级检索的需求。对话级检索对于情感识别、口语对话系统和对话语音合成等任务至关重要。

Method: S2Dialog由三个核心组件构成：(1) 对话级文本检索器，编码对话的文本模态为对话级表示；(2) 对话级声学检索器，编码对话的声学模态为对话级表示；(3) 对话级文本-声学对比学习，对齐语义和风格相似的对话并区分不相关的对话，以增强多模态检索能力。

Result: 在多模态对话数据集DailyTalk上进行的大量实验表明，S2Dialog取得了出色的检索性能，显著优于现有方法。

Conclusion: S2Dialog通过统一的对话级多模态范式，有效解决了对话级语义-风格检索的问题，验证了对话级表征学习与跨模态对比学习在多模态对话检索中的有效性。

Abstract: Multimodal dialogue retrieval aims to retrieve dialogues from multimodal dialogue banks that are similar to a target dialogue in terms of both textual semantics and acoustic conversational styles. Such dialogue-level retrieval is crucial for many dialogue-related tasks, including Emotion Recognition in Conversation, Spoken Dialogue Systems, and Conversational Speech Synthesis, where external dialogue examples can provide valuable semantic and stylistic references. However, existing retrieval methods are still largely limited to utterance-level or unimodal matching, and often fail to capture the global semantic coherence and stylistic consistency of an entire dialogue. To address this gap, we propose S2Dialog, a unified framework for dialogue-level semantic-style retrieval from multimodal dialogue banks. Specifically, S2Dialog consists of a Dialogue-level Textual Retriever and a Dialogue-level Acoustic Retriever, which encode the textual and acoustic modalities of a dialogue into dialogue-level representations, respectively. To further enhance multimodal retrieval, we introduce Dialogue-level Textual-Acoustic Contrastive Learning, which aligns semantically and stylistically similar dialogues while distinguishing unrelated ones. Extensive experiments on the multimodal dialogue dataset DailyTalk demonstrate that S2Dialog achieves outstanding retrieval performance.

</details>


### [89] [HERMES: a multi-agent framework for structured knowledge extraction from ultra-long documents in geoscience](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14055)
*Ziqi Song,Zongyuan Xiang,James G. Ogg,Bruce S. Lieberman,Gabi Ogg,Natalia López Carranza,Wen Du,Yufei Ye,Shuan Li,Zhong Peng,Shaoqi Yu,Juye Wei,Ying Zhou,Jieping Ye,Jiang Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了HERMES，一个可扩展的多智能体框架，用于从超长科学文献中提取结构化数据。该系统成功应用于55卷《古无脊椎动物论丛》，生成了包含32,277个化石分类实体和451,878个属性的结构化数据库，实体和属性提取的F1分数分别约为0.90和0.91，相比纯人工基线效率提升约六倍，并在古地磁学和地球化学领域表现出跨域泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地学领域的权威科学知识仍大量存在于传统专著和历史文献中，其非结构化文本和复杂版面布局严重阻碍了计算访问和大规模知识整合，亟需一种可扩展的自动化解决方案来将这些宝贵的历史文献转化为机器可读的结构化数据。

Method: HERMES采用协调型大语言模型作为核心，在统一的文档级提取流程中整合领域约束、验证规则和证据追踪，系统性地解析文本、表格、插图和标题等多模态内容，实现超长科学文档的结构化数据提取。

Result: 在55卷《古无脊椎动物论丛》上生成了包含32,277个化石分类实体和451,878个属性的结构化数据库（已在线发布）；各化石类群的提取性能稳定（实体平均F1约0.90，属性平均F1约0.91）；每卷的处理效率相比纯人工基线提升约六倍；在古地磁学和地球化学领域的零训练迁移评估中展现出良好的跨域泛化能力。

Conclusion: 该工作为将历史科学文献转化为遵循FAIR原则的结构化数据提供了可行路径，为数据密集型学科和大规模知识整合构建了可持续的基础设施支持。

Abstract: Authoritative scientific knowledge in geoscience remains largely trapped in legacy monographs and historical literature, where unstructured text and complex layouts hinder computational access. We introduce HERMES, a scalable multi-agent framework that extracts structured data from ultra-long scientific documents. Using a coordinating large language model, HERMES integrates domain constraints, validation rules and evidence tracing within a unified document-level extraction process that incorporates parsed text, tables, figures and captions. Applied to the 55-volume Treatise on Invertebrate Paleontology, the system produced a structured database of 32,277 fossil taxonomic entities and 451,878 attributes, released online at https://treatise.geolex.org. Extraction performance remained stable across fossil groups (average F1 scores of approximately 0.90 for entities and 0.91 for attributes), improving per-volume efficiency approximately sixfold relative to the tested fully manual baseline. Evaluation in palaeomagnetism and geochemistry, conducted without additional model training, demonstrated transfer across distinct geoscience domains. This work provides a practical pathway to transform historical scientific literature into FAIR-oriented structured data, offering a sustainable infrastructure for data-intensive disciplines and large-scale knowledge integration.

</details>


### [90] [The conditional superiority of fast silicon sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14079)
*Nickolas Hock Yuen Lam,Ji Xuan Voo,Xiangyu Ma*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究比较了"快速"与"慢速"两种硅采样模式在新加坡全国代表性样本中的算法保真度，发现硅采样整体上仍处于早期发展阶段需谨慎使用，但快速模式在计算效率和算法保真度上均优于慢速模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 硅采样（silicon sampling）有时能产生令人惊讶的较好人口估计，但研究者关心快速执行的硅采样是否会削弱这种保真度。作者旨在评估是否可以通过更快的采样方式保持甚至提升估计质量。

Method: 作者在新加坡全国代表性调查受访者样本中，系统比较了"快速"与"慢速"两种硅采样模式的算法保真度，评估它们对人口均值的估计能力、观点方差的估算准确性以及潜在情境空间的保持程度，同时比较两者在计算资源和运行时间上的效率差异。

Result: 研究发现：(1) 使用当代前沿模型的硅采样仍处于早期发展阶段，应谨慎使用；(2) 硅样本虽能对人口均值提供中等保真的估计，但持续低估观点方差并扭曲人类观点背后的潜在情境空间；(3) 在存在上述局限的前提下，快速硅采样模式在计算资源和运行时间上显著更高效，且在算法保真度上单调优于慢速模式。

Conclusion: 快速硅采样模式相对于传统慢速模式具有相对优越性，既节省资源又提升保真度，但整体而言硅采样方法仍不够成熟，需谨慎对待其估计结果，不能作为成熟的人口估计工具直接使用。

Abstract: Silicon sampling can produce surprisingly good population estimates at times. Does doing it fast attenuate such fidelity? In this study, we extend and assess ongoing work in silicon sampling by comparing the algorithmic fidelity of "fast" and "slow" modes of silicon sampling among a nationally representative sample of Singaporean survey respondents. We find that silicon sampling with contemporary frontier models remains a method in early development to be used only with great caution. While silicon samples are able to produce moderately faithful estimates of population means, they continue to understate opinion variance and distort the latent contextual space behind human opinions. Conditional on such limitations, we find "fast" modes of silicon sampling to be relatively superior to traditional "slow" modes of silicon sampling. Fast silicon sampling is significantly more efficient in compute resources and run-time while being monotonically superior to slower modes of sampling in algorithmic fidelity.

</details>


### [91] [Leading-Silence Augmentation and Multi-Stage Synthetic Supervision for the Second MLC-SLM Challenge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14150)
*Kexin Shi,Renhe Sun,Yuge Huang,Ximeng Wang,Jiayi Zhou,Jian Liu,Malu Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文参加第二届MLC-SLM挑战赛，针对多语言对话语音的两个任务（说话人区分与识别、对话语音理解）提出优化方案：任务1通过随机前导静音裁剪、时间戳修正和EMA训练策略微调VibeVoice-ASR-7B，将tcpMER从18.30%降至16.73%；任务2通过合成问答对、噪声过滤和分布匹配增强微调Qwen3-Omni-30B-A3B-Instruct，将准确率从83.0%提升至86.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 第二届MLC-SLM挑战赛要求在不提供语音段边界和说话人标签的完整未分段多语言对话上进行说话人区分/识别及对话语音理解，且任务2不提供问答训练集，这需要开发无需oracle信息即可完成任务的训练与推理方案。

Method: 任务1：对VibeVoice-ASR-7B采用三种技术——随机前导静音裁剪、一致的时间戳校正以及指数移动平均（EMA）训练策略。任务2：通过多模态候选生成、静音音频过滤和分布匹配增强构建合成问答对，并微调Qwen3-Omni-30B-A3B-Instruct进行带标签的直接回答（tagged direct answering）。

Result: 任务1评估集上，裁剪将tcpMER从18.30%降至17.27%，EMA进一步将其降至16.73%。任务2评估集上，联合应用分布匹配增强和带标签直接回答使准确率从83.0%提升至86.0%。

Conclusion: 所提出的两套方案均在各自任务上取得显著性能提升，验证了随机静音裁剪+EMA对ASR说话人系统鲁棒性的增强作用，以及合成数据增强+分布匹配对无监督问答训练的有效性。

Abstract: The second Multilingual Conversational Speech Language Model (MLC-SLM) Challenge evaluates two tasks over complete, unsegmented multilingual conversations: speaker diarization and recognition (Task 1) and conversational speech understanding (Task 2). Neither task provides oracle utterance boundaries or speaker labels at evaluation, and Task 2 provides no question-answer training set. For Task 1, we fine-tune VibeVoice-ASR-7B with random leading-silence cropping, consistent timestamp correction, and an exponential moving average (EMA) training strategy. For Task 2, we construct synthetic question-answer pairs through multimodal candidate generation, silent-audio filtering, and distribution-matched augmentation, and fine-tune Qwen3-Omni-30B-A3B-Instruct for tagged direct answering. On the Task 1 evaluation set, cropping reduces tcpMER from 18.30% to 17.27%, and EMA further reduces it to 16.73%. On the Task 2 evaluation set, jointly applying distribution-matched augmentation and tagged direct answering raises accuracy from 83.0% to 86.0%.

</details>


### [92] [How Much Do Legal RAG Systems Still Hallucinate?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14210)
*Souvick Das,Sallam Abualhaija,Domenico Bianculli*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文对八个法律领域的检索增强生成（RAG）系统在两个法律语料库（英文GDPR和法国民事法）上的幻觉行为进行了细粒度分析，采用声明级和答案级评估，发现幻觉问题依然普遍存在（最佳系统低于10%，最差系统近半数回答存在幻觉），并发现含有错误前提的问题会引发更高的幻觉率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 法律领域的RAG系统中幻觉问题尤为严重，因为缺乏依据的回答可能导致严重后果。然而此前缺乏对法律RAG系统幻觉行为的系统性、细粒度分析，因此需要更深入地理解幻觉在何种条件下发生、严重程度如何，以及不同系统间的表现差异。

Method: 对八个法律RAG系统在GDPR（英文）和法国民事法（法文）两个语料库上进行评估，采用声明级（claim-level）和答案级（answer-level）两种粒度的评价标准，分析幻觉密度（hallucination density）和严重程度（severity），并跨问题类别和用户角色（personas）进行表现分析，同时在142个由法律专家手工编写的独立问题上验证发现。

Result: 幻觉在法律RAG系统中依然普遍存在，最佳系统的幻觉响应率低于10%，而最差系统近半数响应存在幻觉。此外，包含错误前提（false-premise）的问题——即需要系统拒绝的含有不正确假设的问题——在手工编写的测试问题上产生了较高的幻觉率。

Conclusion: 法律RAG系统的幻觉问题尚未得到有效解决，尤其是在面对含有错误前提的问题时表现尤为薄弱。这提示未来的研究需要更加关注系统对错误假设的识别与拒绝能力，而非仅优化答案的生成质量。

Abstract: Hallucination is a major challenge for retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) systems in the legal domain, where ungrounded answers can lead to serious consequences. To better understand this problem, we conduct a fine-grained analysis of hallucination behavior in eight legal RAG systems across two legal corpora, the GDPR (in English) and a national civil law (in French). Using claim-level and answer-level evaluation, we report on hallucination density and severity, analyze performance across question categories and user personas, and validate our findings on an independent set of 142 legal-expert-authored questions. Our results show that hallucinations remain pervasive, ranging from less than 10% of responses for the best-performing systems to nearly half in the worst case. We further find that false-premise questions, containing incorrect assumptions that must be rejected, produce high hallucination rates on the manually-drafted questions.

</details>


### [93] [The More Popular, The Harder to Forget: Adaptive Popularity for LLM Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14229)
*Anna Borisiuk,Andrey Savchenko,Alexander Panchenko,Elena Tutubalina*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出AdaPop（自适应流行度）方法，利用事实的流行度/频率信息来指导LLM遗忘，通过自适应梯度调节实现更彻底的遗忘和更少的泄漏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM遗忘方法对所有事实施加统一的梯度压力，忽略了训练数据频率的差异。然而，流行事实在预训练中被记忆更深、抵抗遗忘更强，需要针对性的处理策略。

Method: AdaPop结合局部token置信度与基于外部代理（如Wikidata站点链接、LLM作为评委）推导的逐事实流行度依赖指数，并通过双上升（dual-ascent）控制器自动调节保持集（retain）惩罚的平衡，每个epoch动态调整。

Result: 在三个模型家族和两个基准上，AdaPop在释义查询下泄漏遗忘内容比竞争方法少约5倍，在对抗性改写下少约1.6倍。内部指标显示遗忘集隐藏状态比预遗忘模型的移动更远，而保持集表示保持接近。

Conclusion: AdaPop通过考虑事实流行度差异实现了更有效的LLM遗忘，显著降低了遗忘内容在变体查询下的泄漏，同时保持了保留集的完整性。

Abstract: Popular facts are memorised more deeply during pretraining and resist removal longer than rare ones, yet existing LLM unlearning methods apply uniform gradient pressure regardless of training-data frequency. We propose the AdaPop (Adaptive Popularity) method, which combines local token confidence with a per-fact popularity-dependent exponent derived from an external proxy (e.g., Wikidata sitelinks, LLM-as-Judge), and automates the forget-retain balance via a dual-ascent controller that adjusts the retain penalty each epoch. Across three model families and two benchmarks, AdaPop leaks ~5x less forgotten content than competing methods under paraphrased queries and ~1.6x less under adversarial reformulations. We support our analysis with internal metrics: under our method, forget-set hidden states move further from the pre-unlearning model's states than under other methods, while retain-set representations remain close.

</details>


### [94] [SimpleOPD: Simple Tokenizer-Agnostic On-Policy Distillation for Long-Context Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14277)
*Haonan He,Haodi Lei,Yun Luo,Haoran Zhang,Shunkai Zhang,Yizhuo Li,Shengji Tang,Zhilin Wang,Runzhe Zhan,Lei Bai,Ganqu Cui,Fangchen Yu,Yafu Li,Peng Ye,Ning Ding,Yu Cheng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究将长上下文推理教师模型（SU-01）的证明推理能力通过在线策略蒸馏（OPD）迁移到短上下文学生模型中，提出了共享文本空间对齐、学生参考KL损失以及终止令牌优势掩码等策略，在多项数学和科学基准上取得了显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线策略蒸馏（OPD）是迁移推理能力的有效方法，但从长上下文推理教师迁移到短上下文学生时面临分词器不匹配、师生分布不匹配、响应长度爆炸和训练不稳定等实际挑战，需要针对性地解决这些问题。

Method: 1) 在共享文本空间中进行OPD，仅对齐师生分词器下占据相同文本跨度的令牌；2) 引入学生参考KL损失，约束学生不过度偏离初始策略；3) 对特殊终止令牌（如</think>和<|im_end|>）的优势进行掩码，以缓解师生分布不匹配并促进稳定的长度增长。

Result: 在同族和异族学生模型（Qwen3、Qwen3.5、Intern-S2、GLM-4.7、Gemma-4）上均获得一致的数学推理提升，尤其在自然语言数学证明方面。Intern-S2-Preview在ProofBench上提升21.2分达到55.2，超越Gemini-2.5-Pro；同时在HLE和HiPhO等科学基准上也取得改进，表明迁移的推理能力具有跨领域泛化性。

Conclusion: 通过针对分词器不匹配、长度爆炸和分布偏移等问题的针对性策略，OPD能够有效地将长上下文模型的推理能力迁移到短上下文学生模型，且迁移的推理能力不仅限于数学领域，还能泛化到更广泛的科学推理任务。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) offers a promising way to transfer reasoning capabilities from stronger teacher models, but applying it to long-context reasoning teachers and short-context students introduces practical challenges, including tokenizer mismatch, teacher-student distribution mismatch, response length explosion, and training instability. In this work, we study this setting by transferring proof-reasoning capabilities from the long-context reasoning model SU-01 to short-context student models. To handle tokenizer differences, we perform OPD in a shared text space and align only tokens that occupy identical text spans under the student and teacher tokenizers. To mitigate the problem of excessive generation length and frequent truncation, we introduce a student reference KL loss and mask the advantages of special termination tokens such as </think> and <|im_end|>. This strategy constrains the student from drifting excessively from its initial policy, thereby mitigating the teacher-student distribution mismatch problem and fostering steady length growth. Experiments on both same-family and different-family student models, including Qwen3, Qwen3.5, Intern-S2, GLM-4.7, Gemma-4, show consistent gains in mathematical reasoning, especially natural-language math proving. Notably, Intern-S2-Preview improves by 21.2 points on ProofBench, reaching 55.2 and surpassing Gemini-2.5-Pro. It also improves on science benchmarks such as HLE and HiPhO, suggesting that OPD transfers reasoning capabilities that generalize beyond the mathematical training domain.

</details>


### [95] [Envs-FORGE: Frontier-Optimized Reward-Grounded Environment Synthesis for Agent RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14312)
*Xiaojun Wu,Cehao Yang,Honghao Liu,Xueyuan Lin,Zhichao Shi,Hao Zhou,Xuhui Jiang,Chengjin Xu,Jia Li,Jian Guo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Envs-FORGE，一种将验证器奖励转化为逐种子环境合成动作的提示策略，用于终端智能体的强化学习训练环境生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有固定配方（如few-shot、Self-Instruct、Evol-Instruct）对每个种子应用相同的提示策略，无法根据当前策略的实际能力动态调整任务难度，导致训练环境在难度和有效性上无法满足强化学习的需求。

Method: Envs-FORGE首先估计种子通过率，然后评估围绕目标学习前沿的六种投影方向动作，并通过逐种子混合整数线性规划（MILP）选择条件生成动作。所选动作驱动指令、fixtures、oracle解决方案、测试和Docker环境的同步重写，仅经金标准验证的bundle进入RL训练。MILP形式还支持可选的软技能覆盖用于组合规划。

Result: 在Qwen 3.5 35B上，Envs-FORGE在tb-core上Pass@1较Base提升9.2个百分点（40.0%→49.2%），在tb-2.0上提升6.4个百分点（23.0%→29.4%），分别超过最强固定配方基线2.4和2.1个百分点。SWE-bench Verified达到77.1%（Base为73.4%），在4B-35B模型上tb-core提升6.8-9.2个百分点。

Conclusion: Envs-FORGE通过动态环境合成显著优于固定配方策略，在相同训练规模和操作成本下（100个验证环境和2.27M-2.88M合成token）取得一致的性能提升，且代码已开源。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) for terminal agents needs executable training environments with reliable rewards and useful difficulty. Fixed recipes such as few-shot, Self-Instruct, and Evol-Instruct apply the same prompting policy to every seed, even when the current policy would benefit from a harder, easier, or simply different task. We present Envs-FORGE, a prompting policy that converts verifier rewards into per-seed environment-synthesis actions. Envs-FORGE estimates seed pass rates, scores six projection--direction actions around a target learning frontier, and solves a per-seed mixed-integer linear program (MILP) to choose the action that conditions generation. The selected action drives synchronized rewriting of the instruction, fixtures, oracle solution, tests, and Docker environment; only gold-verified bundles enter RL training. The indexed MILP form also supports optional soft skill coverage for portfolio planning. On Qwen 3.5 35B, Envs-FORGE improves Pass@1 over Base by 9.2 percentage points on tb-core (40.0% to 49.2%) and 6.4 points on tb-2.0 (23.0% to 29.4%), exceeding the strongest fixed-recipe baseline by 2.4 and 2.1 points. It reaches 77.1% on SWE-bench Verified versus 73.4% for Base, and improves tb-core by 6.8--9.2 points across the evaluated 4B--35B models. All synthesis methods export 100 verified environments and use 2.27M--2.88M synthesis tokens, placing the comparison at the same downstream training-set size and the same operational scale. The source code is available at https://github.com/DataArcTech/DataArc-SynData-Toolkit/.

</details>


### [96] [Local and Global Regimes of Geometric Complexity in Language Model Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14361)
*Arwa Osman,Marco Baroni,Iuri Macocco*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了词汇多样性（数据集中唯一末词项的数量）如何影响语言模型表示的内在维度（ID）估计，发现了一个尺度依赖的双重状态转变：低词汇多样性时词数越少ID越高，高词汇多样性时词数越多ID越高，并推导出一个无参数的精确公式来预测转变点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 内在维度（ID）被广泛用于探测语言模型的表示复杂性，但现有工作中ID的差异究竟反映语言本身的属性还是数据集构建方式的人为产物（artefact）尚不清楚。作者聚焦于词汇多样性这一具体因素，旨在厘清ID估计的可靠性与解释边界。

Method: 作者系统地操纵数据集中词汇多样性（唯一末词项数量），在多个尺度上测量不同条件下的ID估计值，观察ID随词汇多样性变化的规律。他们发现存在一种尺度依赖的转变，并推导出一个精确且无参数的公式来计算反转发生的临界点，并将该公式的预测与实验观察到的转变点进行对比验证。

Result: 实验发现了一个双状态转变：在低词汇多样性条件下，唯一末词较少的条件反而产生更高的ID；而在高词汇多样性条件下，唯一末词更多的条件产生更高的ID，即排序发生反转。推导出的无参数公式在所有测试尺度上都能精确匹配观察到的转变点。

Conclusion: 该研究一方面警示研究者需要谨慎解读ID作为表征复杂度的直观指标，因为ID差异可能源于数据集构建方式而非语言本质；另一方面，这两个ID状态区域的发现揭示了LLM中语言数据组织的一般性原则，为理解模型内部流形结构提供了新视角。

Abstract: Intrinsic dimensionality (ID) is widely used to probe the representational complexity of language models, but it remains unclear whether ID differences reflect properties of language itself or artefacts of how the underlying dataset was constructed. In this paper, we focus specifically on how lexical diversity, the number of unique last-token items present in a dataset, affects ID estimates of that dataset. We find a scale-dependent transition between two regimes: at low lexical diversity, conditions with fewer unique final words produce higher ID, while at high lexical diversity, this ordering reverses, and conditions with more unique words produce higher ID. We derive an exact, parameter-free formula for the point at which this reversal occurs, which matches the observed transition point at every scale tested. On the one hand, our results highlight how care must be taken when interpreting the intrinsic dimensionality of a set of representations as a straightforward cue of their complexity. On the other hand, our discovery of the two ID regimes reveals a general principle of organisation of linguistic data in LLMs that sheds new light on their inner manifold structures.

</details>


### [97] [A Survey of Large Models in Sports](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14377)
*Yichen Xu,Jianzhe Ma,Chuhan Wang,Zhonghao Cao,Liangyu Chen,Wenxuan Wang,Qin Jin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文对体育运动领域中的大规模模型（尤其是多模态大语言模型）进行了一次全面综述，涵盖不同参与者群体的任务与应用、体育数据集与基准的详细分析，以及当前挑战与未来方向的批判性讨论，旨在为大模型驱动的体育智能研究建立基础，并提供了开源GitHub仓库。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 体育在全球范围内日益受到关注，是促进身体健康、文化交流、社会联系和经济增长的重要力量。与此同时，大型模型（尤其是多模态大语言模型）的快速发展展现出在体育理解、分析和交互等多元领域的巨大变革潜力，但目前缺乏对这一交叉领域的系统性综述，亟需整合梳理。

Method: 本文采用系统性的综述方法，围绕三个主要维度展开：(i) 梳理不同参与者群体（如运动员、教练、裁判、观众、组织者等）所涉及的任务与应用；(ii) 对体育相关数据集和基准进行详细分析与归纳；(iii) 对当前研究中存在的挑战进行批判性讨论，并展望未来研究方向。

Result: 本文提供了一个全面的体育大模型综述框架，系统整理了体育领域中的任务分类、数据资源和评估基准，揭示了该领域的研究现状与空白，并建立了公开的GitHub资源仓库以持续追踪和汇总相关研究进展。

Conclusion: 该综述为大模型驱动的体育智能研究奠定了系统性基础，明确了当前的技术挑战（如多模态数据融合、运动语义理解、实时性等问题）和未来发展方向，有助于推动大模型在体育领域的理论与实践应用。

Abstract: Sports have witnessed growing global enthusiasm in recent years, serving as a vital force for physical health, cultural exchange, social connection, and economic growth. The rapid advancement of large models, particularly (multimodal) large language models (M)LLMs, has demonstrated transformative potential to reshape sports understanding, analysis, and interaction across diverse domains. This paper presents a comprehensive survey of large models in sports, including (i) an overview of tasks and applications across different participant groups; (ii) a detailed analysis of sports-related datasets and benchmarks; and (iii) a critical discussion of current challenges and future directions. Our goal is to establish a foundation for advancing research and practical development of large-model-driven sports intelligence. An open-source GitHub repository is maintained at: https://github.com/Road2Redemption/Awesome_Large_Models_In_Sports1.

</details>


### [98] [Information Satisfaction: A Reader-Centered Axis for Summarization Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14457)
*Isabel Cachola,William Walden,Reno Kriz,Mark Dredze*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文指出当前摘要评估指标（如ROUGE、BERTScore甚至LLM-as-judge）无法衡量摘要对个体用户的信息实用性，提出用读者背景（人设）作为补充信号，并通过扰动测试和专家人工评估证明现有指标在信息满意度测量上与人判断一致性差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有摘要评估指标聚焦于通用质量（如ROUGE、BERTScore）或特定属性（如可读性、事实性），但忽略了摘要对特定用户的信息实用性。查询聚焦摘要只部分捕捉需求，但用户很少在简短查询中完整表达需求，而读者的背景/人设相对稳定，能补充缺失上下文，是评估摘要是否满足读者需求的实际信号。

Method: 1) 设计扰动测试，检验流行摘要评估指标对信息内容差异和人物背景差异的敏感性；2) 进行专家人工评估实验，基于特定人物的背景和使用场景，测量人类对摘要信息满意度的偏好判断。

Result: 发现包括强LLM-as-judge指标在内的许多流行指标无法通过基本信息内容扰动测试；传统指标和基于LLM的指标都无法充分衡量信息满意度，与人类判断的一致性较差。

Conclusion: 现有的摘要评估指标（无论是传统还是基于LLM的）均不足以衡量摘要对个体用户的信息满意度，需要开发考虑到读者人设和背景的新评估方法。

Abstract: The majority of work on summarization evaluation focuses on general summary quality (e.g., ROUGE, BERTScore) or specific desired properties (e.g., readability, factuality). However, these metrics fail to measure the utility of a summary to an individual user. For example, a biomedical researcher learning about the latest vaccine research will have different informational needs from a family doctor. Query-focused summarization captures part of this need, but in practice, users rarely state everything relevant in a query: a single short query is likely inadequate to distinguish the needs of a researcher from those of a physician. By contrast, a reader's background or persona (their role and expertise) is comparatively stable across queries and recovers much of this missing context, which makes it a practical signal for assessing whether a summary satisfies that reader's needs. In this work, we assess how sensitive popular summarization metrics are to both informational and persona differences, and find that many popular metrics, including strong LLM-as-judge metrics, fail basic perturbation tests of informational content. We additionally conduct an expert human evaluation, measuring summary preferences based on information satisfaction given a specific person's background and use case. We find that both traditional and LLM-based metrics are insufficient measures of information satisfaction and agree poorly with human judgment.

</details>


### [99] [You Only Pass Once: Answering and Abstaining Together in a Single Forward Pass of a Frozen Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14465)
*Ziyang Luo,Zhongyao Chu,Xinjie He,Youting Wang,Xukui Qin,Runxiong Wu,Yan-Syuan Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出YOPO系统，通过训练一个小型网络从steering后的残差流重建pre-steering状态，使得冻结语言模型在单次前向传播中同时完成推理、steering和abstention（弃权），克服了steering写入干扰sufficiency方向读取的问题，在多种模型规模和十余个骨干网络上超越了两次前向传播的基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 冻结语言模型在推理任务上有两个耦合缺陷：一是未充分利用残差流中已编码的证据，二是在输入信息不足时无法检测并会产生臆造（confabulation）。已有两条研究线分别通过conditional steering probe（写入残差流以恢复推理准确率）和zero-shot sufficiency direction（读取残差流以判断信息是否充分）来解决，但两者在同一次前向传播中会相互干扰，而分别执行则需要两次前向传播导致推理成本翻倍。

Method: 保持sufficiency direction固定不变，训练一个小型网络以最小化(steered, clean)对上的均方误差（无需sufficiency标签），从steering后的残差重建pre-steering残差，再在重建结果上读取sufficiency direction。由此实现单次前向传播中同时完成回答、steering和abstention。采用Qwen2.5（1.5B/3B/7B）作为冻结骨干，并在十个骨干、六个模型家族上验证。

Result: 在端到端的三路准确率上，1.5B alphaNLI从冻结基线的0.375提升至0.798，超过两倍；单次前向传播在所有规模上均优于两次前向基准（0.798/0.830/0.893 vs 0.753/0.790/0.863），并在十个骨干和六个模型家族上保持一致优势。作者还绘制了容量迁移前沿曲线，并进行了source-side审计，发现了alphaNLI构造中泄漏的表面伪影，因此架构结论基于原生标签复制（SQuAD2、RepLiQA、MuSiQue）验证。在标准四域套件上，作者贡献了首个answer-or-abstain基准，其gate在所有域内数据集上表现最佳，且label-free direction是唯一能在域迁移中存活的gate族。

Conclusion: YOPO证明可以通过残差重建解耦steering写入与sufficiency读取的相互干扰，在冻结骨干的单次前向传播中同时实现推理、引导和弃权，且在准确率和成本上均优于两遍参考方案。同时强调"abstention不应被训练进去"的原则，并揭示了数据集构造中表面伪影对评估的影响，呼吁用原生标签进行验证。

Abstract: A frozen language model on reasoning tasks has two coupled weaknesses: it under-uses evidence its own residual stream already encodes, and it fails to detect when the input is insufficient to answer, so it confabulates. This paper consolidates two research lines that address these on the same residual stream: a conditional steering probe writes the stream at mid-stack layers and recovers reasoning accuracy from a frozen backbone, and a zero-shot sufficiency direction reads the stream and abstains when information is insufficient. Deployed in one forward pass they interfere: the steering write shifts the state the direction reads, costing up to 8 AUROC points of cross-domain transfer on small models; a separate clean pass doubles inference cost. We keep the direction fixed and train a small network to reconstruct the pre-steering residual from the steered one -- mean-squared error on (steered, clean) pairs, no sufficiency labels -- and read the direction on the reconstruction. The resulting system, YOPO (You Only Pass Once), answers, steers, and abstains in one forward pass of a frozen Qwen2.5 backbone (1.5B/3B/7B). End to end, three-way accuracy more than doubles the frozen baseline (0.375->0.798 on 1.5B alphaNLI) and one pass beats the two-pass reference at every scale (0.798/0.830/0.893 vs 0.753/0.790/0.863) and on ten backbones across six model families. We chart the capacity-transfer frontier quantifying the principle that abstention should not be trained in; a source-side audit catches our own alphaNLI construction leaking a surface artifact, so architectural claims are anchored on native-label replications (SQuAD2, RepLiQA, MuSiQue); and on the standard four-domain suite we contribute, to our knowledge, the first answer-or-abstain benchmark, where our gate tops every in-domain dataset and the label-free direction is the only gate family to survive domain transfer.

</details>


<div id='cs.GT'></div>

# cs.GT [[Back]](#toc)

### [100] [What preferences can - and cannot - predict in multi-agent online learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13810)
*Omar Abbadi,Rida Laraki,Panayotis Mertikopoulos*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文研究了博弈中基于序关系/偏好的解概念与无遗憾学习动态（如FTRL）长期行为之间的关联，探讨偏好图（博弈的组合数据）在多大程度上决定学习动态的结果。作者证明了动态稳定集合的骨架必须是偏好稳定的，并进一步探讨其逆命题：在子博弈情形下偏好可刻画渐近稳定性，但在一般情形下该等价关系失效（构造了一个3人博弈的反例），最后通过"聚合偏离韧性"这一基于收益的条件弥合了这一差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解博弈论中静态的偏好/序关系解概念（如偏好稳定集）与动态学习过程的长期结果之间的深层联系，阐明博弈的组合结构（偏好图）在多大程度上能够预测无遗憾学习算法的收敛行为，从而为动态稳定性的判定提供基于偏好的简便判据。

Method: 本文采用理论分析和反例构造相结合的方法。首先从理论上证明动态稳定集的骨架（包含的纯策略组合）必然是偏好稳定的；然后在子博弈情形下证明偏好关系可刻画渐近稳定性；接着通过构造一个三玩家博弈的具体反例，展示在一般情形下偏好稳定与动态稳定之间的等价关系崩溃；最后引入"聚合偏离韧性"（resilience under aggregate deviations）这一基于收益的判定条件来保证任意纯策略张成空间的渐近稳定性。

Result: （1）动态稳定集合的骨架必然是偏好稳定的（一个方向成立）；（2）在子博弈情形下，偏好关系与渐近稳定性是等价的；（3）但一般情形下等价关系不成立——给出了一个三玩家博弈的反例，其中存在偏好稳定但其张成空间动态不稳定的集合；（4）通过引入聚合偏离韧性条件，可以保证任意纯策略张成空间的渐近稳定性，从而弥合了偏好判据的不足。

Conclusion: 偏好的组合数据（偏好图）足以在子博弈情形下刻画学习动态的长期行为，但在一般博弈中偏好稳定不足以成为动态稳定的充分判据；聚合偏离韧性作为一个容易验证的基于收益的条件，可以作为判定动态稳定性的有效补充工具。

Abstract: We examine the interplay between ordinal, preference-based solution concepts in games and the long-run behavior of game dynamics, asking in particular to what extent the combinatorial data of a game -- its preference graph -- determine the outcomes of no-regret learning dynamics -- such as follow-the-regularized-leader (FTRL). In one direction, we show that the skeleton of every dynamically stable set (i.e. the set of pure profiles it contains) must also be preferentially stable, that is, it must be closed under profitable deviations. We then ask the converse question: when do preferences determine the long-run behavior of the players' learning dynamics? We begin by showing that preferences characterize asymptotic stability in the case of subgames -- i.e. subsets of pure profiles obtained by restricting players' action sets. Beyond this case however, the equivalence between dynamic and preferential stability collapses: concretely, we construct a three-player game with a preferentially stable set whose span is dynamically unstable, showing in this way that preferences do not suffice as a criterion of dynamic stability. We then bridge this gap via the notion of resilience under aggregate deviations, an easy-to-check payoff-based condition that guarantees asymptotic stability of arbitrary spans of pure strategies.

</details>


### [101] [Equilibrium Pricing in Oligopolistic Data Markets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14018)
*Bhaskar Ray Chaudhury,Jugal Garg,Eklavya Sharma,Jiaxin Song*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文研究寡头数据市场中预算受限买方与战略数据卖方之间的均衡定价问题，揭示了数据非竞争性导致纯纳什均衡可能不存在（甚至1.363-近似NE也不存在），并提出分段线性凸定价方案可实现常数因子（2-近似）的近似稳定性，模拟验证收敛速度和经验近似保证优于最坏情况。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据市场不同于传统商品市场，数据具有非竞争性（non-rivalry），即同一数据集可被多个买方重复使用。这种特性可能颠覆竞争均衡的经典结论，因此需要重新审视数据市场中均衡定价的存在性和可计算性。

Method: 首先建立卖方竞争定价博弈模型，证明在统一（uniform）定价下精确NE不存在且1.363-近似NE也可能不存在；然后放宽定价机制，允许卖方使用分段线性凸定价函数，以换取常数因子近似稳定性的保证。

Result: 证明了（1）在统一定价下，精确NE不保证存在，1.363-近似NE也可能不存在；（2）采用分段线性凸定价方案后，存在一个定价组合使得任何卖方通过偏离到任何统一价格最多只能提升2倍收益（即2-近似NE对抗统一偏离）；（3）仿真显示快速收敛且经验近似比优于2的最坏情况界。

Conclusion: 数据市场的非竞争性本质使得经典竞争均衡框架失效，需借助超出统一定价的灵活定价机制来实现近似均衡；分段线性凸定价在理论上提供常数因子近似保证，在实践中表现优于理论最坏情况。

Abstract: We study equilibrium pricing in oligopolistic data markets with budget-constrained buyers (e.g., machine learning companies purchasing data to improve model accuracy) and strategic data sellers. Sellers compete by setting prices for their datasets, giving rise to a pricing game whose pure Nash equilibria correspond to equilibrium prices. While equilibrium prices are guaranteed for rivalrous goods via competitive equilibrium, we show that the non-rivalry of data fundamentally alters this picture: an exact Nash equilibrium (NE) need not exist, and in fact, 1.363-approximate NE may also not exist under uniform pricing. We therefore investigate relaxed equilibrium notions. Allowing sellers to use beyond-uniform pricing---specifically, piecewise-linear convex pricing functions---guarantees approximate stability within a constant factor: there exists a pricing profile in which no seller can improve revenue by a factor of two by deviating to any uniform price (a 2-approximate NE against uniform deviations). Finally, our simulations demonstrate fast convergence and empirical approximation guarantees that outperform the worst-case bound of 2.

</details>


### [102] [Optimal Pricing and Charging Strategy Design for Non-cooperative Battery Swapping Stations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14167)
*Huanyu Yan,Chenxi Sun,Huanxin Liao,Xiaoying Tang*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文研究了由同一聚合器协调的多个竞争性电池换电站（BSS）在电力市场中的最优定价与充电策略问题，通过构造两阶段层次博弈模型证明子博弈精炼纳什均衡（SPNE）的存在性与唯一性，并基于此提出最大化各BSS利润的策略。基于西安真实数据的12-BSS仿真表明，该策略使单个BSS利润提升至少18.1%，同时充电策略自然实现电网削峰填谷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着越来越多主体参与建设电池换电站（BSS），在竞争性市场中非合作BSS如何最大化自身利润这一问题尚未得到充分研究，需要深入探讨竞争环境下BSS的最优决策。

Method: 构建层次博弈模型：第一阶段BSS在日前市场确定换电价格，第二阶段在实时市场确定最优充电策略；严格证明SPNE的存在性与唯一性；基于唯一SPNE提出最优定价与充电策略；并提出预测误差处理方法应对换电需求波动。

Result: 基于西安真实数据的12-BSS系统仿真显示，所提出的定价与充电策略使单个BSS利润至少提升18.1%；最优充电策略自然实现了电网削峰填谷。

Conclusion: SPNE的唯一性为竞争环境下均衡策略的最优性提供了理论支撑，所提出的方法不仅能提升个体BSS利润，还能通过最优充电策略对电网产生积极的调峰效应，兼顾了经济效益与电网稳定性。

Abstract: Battery swapping is a rapid way to recharge electric vehicles (EVs). As more and more entities are involved in building Battery Swapping Stations (BSSs), how non-cooperative BSSs maximize their profit in a competitive market needs further investigation. In this paper, we focus on a practical scenario where competitive BSSs are coordinated by the same aggregator. To study the optimal pricing and battery charging, we formulate a hierarchical game-theoretic model, where BSSs determine the swapping price in the day-ahead market in the first stage, and then determine the optimal battery charging strategy in the real-time market in the second stage. We rigorously prove the existence and uniqueness of the Subgame Perfect Nash Equilibrium (SPNE). In particular, the uniqueness property provides theoretical support that the strategy under equilibrium is optimal in the competitive environment. Based on the unique SPNE, we propose an optimal pricing and charging strategy for each BSS to maximize profit in the competitive market. A prediction error handling method is also proposed to deal with unexpected fluctuations in swapping demand. Our simulation with a 12-BSS system based on real-life data from Xi'an, China shows that our pricing and charging strategy increases the individual BSS profit by at least 18.1\%, while the optimal charging strategy naturally achieves peak shaving for the power grid.

</details>


### [103] [Individual Rationality in Constrained Hedonic Games: Friends, Enemies, and Neutrals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14461)
*Šimon Schierrreich,Ildikó Schlotter*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文研究了由朋友、敌人和中立关系引发的受限联盟形成博弈，在朋友导向和敌人导向两种偏好细化下，求解满足个体理性（IR）且恰好k个联盟（每个联盟有大小上下界）的划分的计算复杂性。结果表明敌人导向偏好下问题简化为带大小约束的图着色，而朋友导向偏好下复杂性由敌意结构而非友谊决定，其复杂度进一步受大小约束严格程度和关系对称性影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管仅要求个体理性在任意享乐博弈中都是平凡可满足的，但引入联盟数量及大小约束后，判定可行划分是否存在变得计算上难以处理，需要系统地刻画这一问题的复杂度边界。

Method: 作者在朋友/敌人/中立三方关系诱导的享乐博弈中，对朋友导向和敌人导向两种偏好模型分别研究带大小约束的IR划分问题，结合经典复杂度与参数化复杂度分析，考察关系对称/非对称以及大小约束严格程度对复杂度的影响。

Result: 敌人导向偏好下问题等价于带大小约束的图着色问题，复杂度随图着色复杂度而定；朋友导向偏好下问题由敌意结构主导，复杂度取决于大小约束的严格程度与关系对称性，对称情形中若干情况可解，非对称情形中则变为难解，作者完整刻画了导致难解性的友敌结构属性。

Conclusion: 两种偏好模型呈现截然不同的复杂性图景：敌人导向下问题可归结为图着色；朋友导向下复杂性由敌意结构决定，且对称性造成了明显的可解/难解分界。论文在经典与参数化复杂度两个维度上提供了对该问题完整且精确的理解。

Abstract: We study constrained coalition formation in games induced by friends, enemies, and neutrals, under the two standard refinements of additively separable preferences: friend-oriented and enemy-oriented. We ask for partitions that are individually rational (IR), while additionally requiring exactly $k$ non-empty coalitions, each satisfying a prescribed lower and upper bound on its size. Although IR alone is trivial to satisfy for any hedonic game, the size constraints make it computationally intractable to decide whether a feasible partition exists.
  The two models tell strikingly different stories. Under enemy-oriented preferences, the problem collapses to size-constrained graph coloring, and its complexity follows accordingly. Under friend-oriented preferences, however, the picture is far more intricate, and is governed by the enmity structure rather than the friendships. The complexity is further shaped by two factors: how strict the imposed size requirements are, and whether relationships are symmetric or asymmetric, with several cases turning out tractable in the symmetric setting but intractable once asymmetry is allowed. Charting this boundary in terms of both classical and parameterized complexity, we provide a complete understanding of which properties of the friend/enemy structure are responsible for hardness.

</details>


### [104] [Ex-ante versus Ex-post: Egalitarian Facility Location Mechanism Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14499)
*Zohar Barak,Inbal Talgam-Cohen*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文研究设施选址机制设计问题中随机策略证明（strategyproof-in-expectation）机制的公平性界限。在低维空间（ℝ和ℝ²），存在优于确定性机制的随机机制：ℝ中可达到最优ex-ante近似比1，ℝ²中随机旋转角点机制达到1.598，而ex-post目标下界为1.605，揭示了两者严格分离。在高维空间中，任何随机策略证明机制都无法超越确定性独裁机制的o_d(1)改进，且RRCWM机制对两种目标均达到2的近似比，是最优的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 设施选址机制设计是经典的机制设计问题，目标是寻找策略证明（strategyproof）且公平（最小化最大代理成本/博彩主义成本）的机制。确定性机制的最优近似比为2（由独裁机制实现），但随机机制是否能在公平性上有所突破尚不清楚。现有研究集中于ex-post（期望最大成本）评估，而本文提出研究ex-ante（最大期望成本）评估，因其与期望策略证明性在数学结构上天然一致，并系统考察各维度下随机机制在两种评估准则下的理论界限。

Method: 本文采用理论分析的方法：(1) 在ℝ中构建简单机制实现ex-ante最优近似比1；(2) 在ℝ²中设计"随机旋转角点"（Random Rotated Corner）机制，利用随机旋转与角点选取的组合策略，证明其ex-ante近似比至多1.598，并证明ex-post目标下近似比下界为1.605从而实现严格分离；(3) 在高维空间中，通过构造性论证证明任何期望策略证明机制相对于确定性独裁机制最多只能改善o_d(1)；(4) 分析随机旋转坐标中位数（RRCWM）机制，证明其对ex-post和ex-ante目标在任意维度均达到近似比2。

Result: (1) 在ℝ中，存在简单策略证明机制实现ex-ante最优近似比1；(2) 在ℝ²中，"随机旋转角点"机制达到ex-ante近似比至多1.598，优于确定性下界2，而ex-post目标的下界为1.605，证明ex-ante与ex-post目标在ℝ²中严格分离；(3) 在高维空间ℝ^d（d≫1）中，任何期望策略证明机制对目标（无论ex-post还是ex-ante）都无法超越确定性独裁机制的o_d(1)改进；(4) RRCWM机制在任意维度对两种目标均达到近似比2，且在高维中是最优的。

Conclusion: 本文系统刻画了随机策略证明机制在公平性（博彩主义）目标下的近似比上下界，揭示了维度对机制设计能力的关键影响：低维空间中随机化能带来实质性的公平性提升且ex-ante与ex-post评估准则出现严格分离；高维空间中随机化几乎无法改善公平性，确定性独裁机制已接近最优。这项工作深化了对随机机制设计理论的理解，并为设施选址问题中的公平机制设计提供了明确的理论指引。

Abstract: We study the facility location mechanism design problem where $n$ strategic agents report locations in Euclidean space and the mechanism outputs a single facility location. Each agent's cost is its distance from the facility, and our objective is to minimize the egalitarian cost, i.e., the maximum agent cost, in a strategyproof way.
  The optimal deterministic approximation ratio is $2$, achieved by any dictator mechanism. We study the power of randomized strategyproof-in-expectation mechanisms. Prior work has focused on ex-post evaluation, defined as the expected maximum agent cost. We instead study ex-ante evaluation, defined as the maximum expected agent cost, which is naturally aligned with strategyproofness in expectation.
  We establish the following results:
  (1) Low dimensions: Strict ex-ante vs. ex-post separation. In $\mathbb{R}$, we give a simple strategyproof mechanism achieving the optimal ex-ante approximation ratio of $1$. In $\mathbb{R}^2$, we design the "Random Rotated Corner" mechanism, with ex-ante approximation ratio at most $1.598$, breaking the deterministic barrier. For the ex-post objective, we prove a lower bound of $1.605$, yielding a strict separation in $\mathbb{R}^2$.
  (2) High dimensions: Impossibility. In $\mathbb{R}^d$ for $d \gg 1$, we show that no strategyproof-in-expectation mechanism improves on the deterministic dictator mechanism beyond $o_d(1)$. Thus neither ex-post nor ex-ante evaluation yields improved fairness guarantees in high dimensions. An implication is that the "Random Rotation Coordinate-Wise Median" (RRCWM), currently the best known mechanism for the utilitarian objective, is also best possible for the egalitarian objective in high dimension: we show it achieves an approximation ratio of $2$ for both ex-post and ex-ante objectives in $\mathbb{R}^d$ for every $d \ge 1$.

</details>


### [105] [Forging Self-Funded Marketplaces among Strategic Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14548)
*Yuan Deng,Vasilis Gkatzelis,Xizhi Tan,Grigoris Velegkas,Song Zuo*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文提出"自筹资金市场"机制设计问题：如何设计激励机制，使战略型代理人自愿付出努力并形成自筹资金的市场（无需外部预算）。作者将这一问题推广了预算可行机制设计，证明了在完全信息最优基准下无法实现有界近似的诚实拍卖，但提出了一类序贯拍卖，其子博弈完美均衡可实现对数近似；同时引入极大极小份额（MMS）基准并设计了常数近似的拍卖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的预算可行机制设计假设存在一个预先给定预算B，但实际市场往往需要代理人自身产生收入来覆盖成本（即自筹资金）。当代理人的成本c_i未知且可大于或小于收益r_i时，如何设计机制既保证激励相容、个人理性（p_i≥x_i·c_i）又满足预算平衡（总支付≤总收入），是一个尚未被系统研究的问题。

Method: 1) 首先分析第一最优基准（无私人信息下的最优值），证明任何诚实拍卖都无法对该基准实现有界近似，即使在受限设置下也只能达到对数近似的下界；2) 提出一类序贯拍卖机制，证明其子博弈完美均衡能保证对第一最优基准的对数近似；3) 引入一个替代基准——极大极小份额（MMS），用于更好地捕捉市场厚度，并设计一种拍卖使其子博弈完美均衡实现对该基准的常数近似。

Result: 理论结果表明：(1) 对第一最优基准，不存在能实现有界近似的诚实拍卖，且存在对数近似的下界；(2) 所提出的序贯拍卖在子博弈完美均衡下可实现对数近似；(3) 基于MMS基准，所设计的拍卖可实现常数近似。

Conclusion: 本文揭示了自筹资金市场中机制设计的内在困难（成本私有信息与预算平衡约束的组合导致无法逼近第一最优），但通过引入更贴合市场厚度的MMS基准，表明常数近似是可实现的，为自筹资金市场机制设计提供了可行途径和理论保证。

Abstract: We introduce the problem of designing mechanisms that incentivize strategic agents to form self-funded marketplaces. In our model, if agent $i$ exerts effort $x_i\in [0,1]$, they incur a cost of $x_i\cdot c_i$ (where $c_i$ is unknown to the mechanism designer) and they generate revenue $x_i\cdot r_i$; crucially, $c_i$ can be greater or smaller than $r_i$. Each effort profile $\mathbf{x}$ yields value $v(\mathbf{x})$ and the objective is to choose an effort vector that maximizes the value while ensuring that every agent $i$ receives a payment $p_i\geq x_i\cdot c_i$ and that $\mathbf{x}$ is budget-balanced, i.e., $\sum_{i} p_i \leq \sum_{i} x_i\cdot r_i$. This problem generalizes the well-studied budget-feasible mechanism design problem, where the requirement is that $\sum_{i} p_i \leq B$ for some predetermined budget $B$.
  To evaluate the performance of such mechanisms, we first consider the first-best benchmark (the optimal value achievable in the absence of any private information) and show that no truthful auction can achieve a bounded approximation of this benchmark. Also, even in restricted settings, no auction can achieve better than a logarithmic approximation. We complement these results by proposing a class of sequential auctions whose subgame perfect equilibria guarantee a logarithmic approximation of this benchmark. We then introduce an alternative benchmark, the maximin share (MMS), that better captures the thickness of the market and we provide an auction whose subgame perfect equilibria achieve a constant approximation of this benchmark.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [106] [Inducing Reward-Free Judging Rubrics that Reduce Over-Crediting in Agent Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13564)
*Darragh Quinn,David Dylan,Roisin Healy,Fionn Carroll,Maeve Donnelly,Cormac Sheehan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RubricForge 通过反射式进化从少量带标签轨迹中自动生成人类可读的评分标准，用作文本智能体的奖励无关评判器，相比 G-Eval 等通用评判器在降低误判通过率（false-pass rate）方面更可靠，尽管整体一致性提升不显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模评估语言模型智能体时越来越多地依赖第二个语言模型作为自动评判器，因为真实环境奖励昂贵、缓慢或部署时不可用。然而现有评判器（如手写评分标准或微调评判模型）倾向于将流畅但不成功的轨迹误判为成功，缺乏可信度。需要一种将评判标准扎根于真实结果、可被信任的奖励无关代理评判器。

Method: RubricForge 的核心方法是从少量带真实标签的轨迹中通过反射式进化（reflective evolution）演化出评判标准文本，以最大化与环境奖励的一致性为目标；随后冻结该标准，在单次模型调用中应用于未见的轨迹，无需环境访问。整个优化产物是人类可读的文本，每个判定都可归因于具名标准。实验中使用同一个冻结的 7B 模型同时作为智能体和评判器，在 tau-bench（220 次rollout中抽取的173条带标签轨迹）和 WebShop（160条）上进行评估。

Result: RubricForge 的主要收益在于保真度（faithfulness）而非原始一致性。与通用 G-Eval 评判器相比，整体一致性差异不具统计显著性（McNemar p=0.248），绝对分数校准略逊于通用评判器（|err| 差异 -0.048，p=2x10^-4）。但 RubricForge 将失败轨迹误判为成功的比例约为后者的一半（tau-bench 上误判通过率 0.115 vs. 0.173），有三次过度授信被纠正且零反转；在 WebShop 上排名保真度更高（Spearman 0.410 vs. 0.370）。

Conclusion: 对于奖励无关的评判器，误判通过率（false-pass rate）而非总体一致性才是部署时真正相关的指标，因为误判通过会导致有缺陷的智能体被部署上线，而误判失败仅造成重试成本。RubricForge 在此维度上显著优于通用评判器，证明了将评判标准扎根于真实标注结果的演化式方法在奖励无关评估中的价值。

Abstract: Evaluating language-model agents at scale increasingly relies on a second language model as an automatic judge, because the gold signal, an executable environment reward, is expensive, slow, or unavailable at deployment time. Such a judge is a reward-free proxy whose value depends on whether it can be trusted, yet existing judges either hand-write the scoring rubric, as in G-Eval, or fine-tune the judge's weights, and both tend to credit fluent but unsuccessful trajectories as successes. We instead induce the text of an agent-judging rubric from a small set of ground-truth-labeled trajectories, grounding it in true outcomes. We present RubricForge, which evolves a judge rubric by reflective evolution against labeled trajectories to maximize agreement with the environment reward, freezes it, and applies it to held-out trajectories in one model call with no environment access. The optimized artifact is human-readable text, so every verdict is attributable to named criteria. Using one frozen 7B model as both agent and judge, on tau-bench (173 labeled trajectories drawn from 220 rollouts) and WebShop (160), the principal gain is faithfulness rather than raw agreement. The edge over a generic G-Eval judge is not statistically significant (McNemar p = 0.248), and absolute-score calibration marginally favors the generic judge (|err| difference -0.048, p = 2x10^-4). Yet RubricForge over-credits failed trajectories roughly half as often (0.115 vs. 0.173 false-pass rate on tau-bench, with three over-credit catches and zero reversals) and ranks graded WebShop outcomes more faithfully (Spearman 0.410 vs. 0.370). For a reward-free evaluator the false-pass rate, not aggregate agreement, is the deployment-relevant quantity, since a false pass ships a broken agent whereas a false fail merely costs a retry.

</details>


### [107] [Depth-Aware Sensitivity Analysis of Mixture-of-Experts Models via Magnitude-Based Expert Masking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13565)
*Pradeep Kumar Sharma,Shantanu Godbole,Hritvik Shrivastava*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对Qwen3.6-35B-A3B模型（40个MoE层，每层256个专家，top-8路由）进行了系统性的逐层敏感性分析，发现层敏感性呈强深度依赖性：早期层（0-9）和中层（10-29）对专家掩码高度脆弱，而晚期层（30-39）尤其是极晚期层（35-39）可容忍激进的低幅专家掩码。在500-prompt验证集上，极晚期策略（35-39层@50%）仅掩码640个专家即保留419/500个Good+Similar输出，展示了最优的质量/掩码权衡。此外，将top-k路由从8降至6可显著降低墙钟时间且不损失质量，但尚不能与激进专家掩码干净组合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合专家（MoE）架构在保持计算效率的同时扩展了大语言模型规模，但单个MoE层的相对重要性尚未得到充分刻画，特别是在模型压缩场景下缺乏系统性的层间敏感性理解。

Method: 在XLCoST跨语言代码翻译基准上，对Qwen3.6-35B-A3B模型（40个MoE层、每层256个专家、top-8路由）采用基于幅度的专家掩码进行多阶段研究，跨越100/300/500个提示词评估规模，在三台H100 GPU服务器上执行。同时探索了将top-k路由宽度从8降至6的优化。

Result: 发现了层敏感性的深度依赖：早期层（0-9）和中层（10-29）脆弱，晚期层（30-39）尤其极晚期（35-39）容忍激进掩码。均匀全层30%掩码在300-prompt规模仅保留150/300个Good+Similar输出，而晚期聚焦策略保留249-255/300个同时掩码640-1,145个专家。在500-prompt验证集上，极晚期策略（35-39层@50%）保留419/500个Good+Similar输出，仅掩码10,240个专家中的640个。top-k从8降至6在100-prompt探针上显著减少墙钟时间且无Good+Similar损失。

Conclusion: 这些发现为深度感知的MoE专家掩码提供了实证基础，并确立了通向物理权重手术、基于激活的专家评分以及基于训练的恢复的实践路径。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures scale large language models (LLMs) while preserving computational efficiency through sparse activation. Despite their widespread adoption, the relative importance of individual MoE layers remains insufficiently characterized, particularly for model compression. This paper presents a systematic layer-wise sensitivity analysis of the Qwen3.6-35B-A3B model (40 MoE layers, 256 experts per layer, top-8 routing) using magnitude-based expert masking on the XLCoST cross-lingual code translation benchmark. We conduct a multi-phase study spanning 100, 300, and 500 prompt evaluation scales across three H100 GPU servers. Our central finding is that layer sensitivity is strongly depth-dependent: early layers (0-9) and middle layers (10-29) are highly fragile to expert masking, while late layers (30-39), and especially very-late layers (35-39), tolerate aggressive masking of low-magnitude experts. Flat all-layer masking at 30% retains only 150/300 Good+Similar outputs at 300-prompt scale, whereas late-focused policies retain 249-255/300 while masking 640-1,145 experts. On a later 500-prompt held-out validation slice, the narrow very-late policy (layers 35-39 @ 50%) achieves the strongest quality/masked-expert tradeoff among tested candidates, retaining 419/500 Good+Similar outputs while masking only 640 of 10,240 total experts. We additionally characterize top-k routing width reduction from 8 to 6 active experts per token, which shows a large observed wall-clock reduction on a 100-prompt probe with no Good+Similar loss, though it does not yet compose cleanly with aggressive expert masking. These findings provide an empirical foundation for depth-aware MoE expert masking and establish a practical path toward physical weight surgery, activation-based expert scoring, and training-based recovery.

</details>


### [108] [Benchmarking data-driven material models on the classic Treloar dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14063)
*Hagen Holthusen,Moritz Flaschel,Denisa Martonová,Ellen Kuhl*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文使用Treloar经典实验数据，全面基准测试了六种机器学习驱动的超弹性本构建模方法（包括CONN、PANNs、数据指纹技术等），比较了它们的拟合性能、计算成本、超参数敏感性和实现难度，并探讨了预测精度与模型复杂度之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习正在快速改变本构建模领域，涌现出大量基于机器学习的本构方法，但缺乏对这些方法在实际实验数据上的系统比较。作者旨在提供一份公平、可复现的基准测试，帮助研究者理解各方法的优缺点并做出明智选择。

Method: 以Treloar经典单轴、等双轴和纯剪切实验数据为基准，对六种超弹性本构方法进行系统比较，评估指标包括拟合性能、计算成本、超参数敏感性、实现难度、模型参数数量及本构模型求值时间，所有代码和数据公开可复现。

Result: 所有六种方法均能出色地再现基准实验数据。研究未选出单一最佳方法，而是揭示了每种方法各自的优势与局限，并提供了面向实际使用的指导建议。

Conclusion: 在机器学习本构建模领域，不存在普适的最优方法；各方法在拟合能力上均可达到较高水平，但需根据实际应用场景在精度、复杂度和计算效率之间进行权衡。公开源码和数据集为后续研究提供了可复现的基准平台。

Abstract: Machine learning is rapidly reshaping constitutive modeling, offers new ways to learn material behavior directly from experimental data, and challenges long-established modeling paradigms. But with a growing number of machine-learning-based approaches available, how do they compare in practice? In this paper, we use the classic experimental data of Treloar to benchmark popular frameworks for hyperelasticity: (Generalized-Invariant) Constitutive Artificial Neural Networks, Physics-Augmented Neural Networks, (Adaptive) Material Fingerprinting, and Efficient Unsupervised Constitutive Law Identification & Discovery. We compare their fitting performance, computational cost, hyperparameter sensitivity, and ease of implementation. Furthermore, we discuss the trade-offs between predictive accuracy and model complexity. The latter is assessed by quantifying both the number of material parameters in the discovered models and the computational time required to evaluate the constitutive model and its derivatives. The results show that all methods can reproduce the benchmark data remarkably well. Rather than identifying a single winner, we highlight the strengths and limitations of each approach and provide practical guidance for their use. The source code for all six methods, including the training and comparison scripts, as well as all results and data used in this study, is publicly available via https://doi.org/10.5281/zenodo.21915635.

</details>


### [109] [Modular Cognitive Architecture Emerges in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13567)
*Pengrui Han,Jacob Andreas,Evelina Fedorenko,Andrea Gregor de Varda*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究通过对46个任务（涵盖语言、形式推理、社会推理和物理推理四个认知领域）的电路分析，发现大语言模型（LLM）中涌现出与人类大脑相似的模块化架构：在人类大脑中调用相同网络的任务在LLM中调用重叠的神经元，而调用不同网络的任务则调用不同的神经元。这种模块化结构的趋同涌现表明，模块化可能是智能系统的基本属性，而非生物大脑的进化偶然。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类大脑表现出显著的功能专门化，不同网络分别支持语言、形式推理、心智推理和物理推理等能力。作者试图回答一个根本性问题：这种模块化组织是智能系统必须遵循的基本原则，还是生物大脑进化过程中偶然出现的特定现象？通过在LLM（另一种通过完全不同优化过程创建的智能系统）中检验是否存在类似组织，可以获得跨系统的证据。

Method: 采用电路分析（circuit analysis）方法，在N=46个任务上进行研究，这些任务跨越四个认知领域：语言、形式推理、社会推理和物理推理。通过将LLM中神经元的活动模式与人类大脑中的网络组织进行映射对比，检验任务依据人类大脑网络归属而在LLM中是否表现出相应的神经元重叠或分离模式。

Result: 研究发现LLM展现出与人类大脑镜像的模块化架构：在人类中由同一网络支持的任务在LLM中会招募重叠的神经元集合，而由不同网络支持的任务则招募不同的神经元。这种跨系统的结构平行性在46个任务中得到了验证。

Conclusion: 模块化架构在大脑和神经网络中趋同涌现，表明模块化可能是智能系统的一种基本属性，而非生物大脑特有的进化副产品。这一发现对理解智能的本质和AI架构设计具有重要启示。

Abstract: The human brain exhibits a striking degree of functional specialization, with distinct networks supporting language, formal reasoning, reasoning about other minds, and reasoning about the physical world. Is this modular organization a fundamental principle of how intelligent systems must be built, or an evolutionary accident specific to biological brains? Here, we test whether a similar organization emerges in Large Language Models--another class of intelligent systems created through a very different optimization process. Using circuit analyses across N=46 tasks spanning four cognitive domains (language, formal reasoning, social reasoning, physical reasoning), we find that LLMs develop a modular architecture that mirrors the human brain: tasks drawing on the same network in humans recruit overlapping neurons in LLMs, whereas tasks drawing on different networks recruit distinct neurons. The convergent emergence of modularity in brains and neural networks suggests that it may be a fundamental property of intelligent systems.

</details>


### [110] [A Year in LLM Serving: Workload Evolution, Caching and Load-Balancing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13573)
*William Nixon,Jon Durbin,Florian Standhartinger,Haryadi S. Gunawi,Juncheng Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过对Chutes平台一年的生产环境LLM服务追踪数据进行分析，从全局特征化和纵向研究两个角度揭示了真实世界LLM服务工作负载的演化规律和用户-模型交互结构，并将发布完整的一年追踪数据以支持后续研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM服务工作负载研究在规模和范围上存在局限，通常只观察短时间周期，对用户在真实生产环境中与模型的交互方式缺乏可见性，无法充分捕捉工作负载随时间的演化以及用户-模型交互如何塑造生产流量。

Method: 基于Chutes平台一年期的生产追踪数据，从聚合、时间、模型层面和用户层面四个维度对LLM服务工作负载进行分析，同时进行全局特征化和纵向研究，覆盖众多模型和用户（包括热门和长尾模型）。

Result: 揭示了通常被聚合视图隐藏的工作负载演化和用户-模型结构；将发布完整的一年期追踪数据，支持下游研究无需依赖采样或合成生成的工作负载。

Conclusion: 该研究通过大规模真实生产追踪数据弥补了现有LLM服务负载研究的不足，提供了更全面、更长期的工作负载特征理解，并为未来的下游研究提供了宝贵的数据资源。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) serving has become a critical cloud workload, and realistic traces are essential for motivating and benchmarking serving systems. However, existing LLM serving workload studies remain limited in scale and scope. They often observe short time periods and provide limited visibility into how users interact with models in production. As a result, they do not fully capture how LLM serving workloads evolve over time or how user-model interactions shape production traffic.
  In this work, we further the understanding of real-world LLM serving workloads through both a global characterization and a longitudinal study of a one-year production trace from Chutes. Unlike prior studies, our trace captures full production behavior across many models and users, including both popular and long-tail models. We analyze the workload from aggregate, temporal, model-level, and user-level perspectives, revealing workload evolution and user-model structure that are typically hidden behind aggregate views. To support future research, we will release the full one-year trace with the paper, enabling downstream studies of production behavior without relying on sampled or synthetically generated workloads.

</details>


### [111] [Agentao: A Governed Local-First Runtime for Tool-Using LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13574)
*Bo Jin,Qiang Jiao,Xin Tong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Agentao是一个治理优先的本地优先运行时，用于工具使用型LLM智能体。它通过分层架构将模型生成的动作提议与主机授权的执行分离，使权限、状态、协议边界和执行轨迹成为显式运行时抽象，从而构建更可治理、可检查的智能体系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体作为执行系统时引入了多种风险，包括过度权限的动作、弱审计能力、提示注入、工具投毒和不受控的副作用。现有系统缺乏将治理机制作为一等公民的运行时抽象，需要一种能够显式管理权限、状态和执行轨迹的系统架构来解决这些问题。

Method: Agentao采用分层架构，包含主机面对的表面、主机契约、运行时核心、权限中介的工具系统，以及支持内存、重放、插件、技能、子智能体和协议集成的子系统。核心设计是将模型生成的动作提议与主机授权的执行分离，通过权限系统中介所有工具调用，并提供结构化事件接口来记录完整的执行轨迹。

Result: 论文展示了Agentao如何使权限、状态、协议边界和执行轨迹成为显式运行时抽象。虽然不提供正式的安全保证，但该系统展示了构建更可治理、可检查、适合主机控制的本地环境的智能体的具体方法。代码已公开在GitHub上。

Conclusion: Agentao证明了通过将治理机制嵌入运行时架构，可以有效提升LLM智能体的可治理性和可检查性。该工作为构建安全的本地优先智能体运行时提供了实用的设计参考，但也明确指出它没有提供正式的安全保证，暗示了该领域仍需要进一步的安全形式化工作。

Abstract: LLM agents increasingly operate as execution systems that invoke tools, modify local state, use persistent memory, and interact with external protocols. These capabilities make agents useful, but they also introduce risks related to over-privileged actions, weak auditability, prompt injection, tool poisoning, and uncontrolled side effects. This paper presents Agentao, a governed local-first runtime for tool-using LLM agents. Agentao separates model-generated action proposals from host-authorized execution through a layered architecture consisting of host-facing surfaces, a host contract, a runtime core, a permission-mediated tool system, and supporting subsystems for memory, replay, plugins, skills, sub-agents, and protocol integration. We describe the motivation, threat model, design goals, governance model, execution pipeline, and structured event interface of the system. Agentao does not provide formal safety guarantees; rather, it demonstrates how permissions, state, protocol boundaries, and execution traces can be made explicit runtime abstractions for building agents that are more governable, inspectable, and suitable for host-controlled local environments. The code is publicly available at https://github.com/jin-bo/agentao.

</details>


### [112] [Attributing Preprocessing Invariance in Spectral Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14227)
*Dongjun Wei,Hongyi Wu,Yinuo Zou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文质疑了光谱基础模型预处理不变性的评估方式。作者以拉曼基础模型为案例，指出模型内部的归一化层（无学习参数）本身就能将特定预处理差异映射为相同输入，因此模型在跨预处理测试中表现出的"不变性"可能完全归因于归一化本身而非学习。在六个拉曼数据集上，模型并未显著优于其自身的归一化层；训练虽改进了编码器，但仅在变换触及编码器时才被学习到。跨五种模态的多个发布系统中，大多数归一化已能消除此类变换，却被误报为学习所得。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有预训练光谱基础模型通常声称跨预处理不变性，但评估方法（训练-测试不同预处理）可能将归一化层的固有属性误归因于模型学习。作者希望准确区分"学到的不变性"与"归一化自身带来的不变性"，纠正领域内的错误表述。

Method: 作者证明：当归一化使用每条光谱自身统计量时，两条不同预处理的光谱映射到同一向量，当且仅当一条是另一条的正倍数加常数。随后将编码器与归一化单独作对照，在六个拉曼数据集上对比模型完整输出与仅归一化的输出；通过受控实验验证训练是否真正学到忽视变换；并用数值测试判定给定归一化能消除哪些变换；最后在多个发布系统上复现比较。

Result: 在六个拉曼评估数据集上，模型并未可测地优于其自身归一化层；模型确实优于原始光谱，但归一化单独也能达到同样效果。训练确实提升了编码器相对随机初始化的表现，但受控实验表明它只学习忽略能到达编码器的变换。跨五种模态的大量已发布系统中，多数归一化已能消除此类变换，且多个系统将这些不变性误称为学习所得；复现其中两个系统的比较，同样没有发现增益。

Conclusion: 光谱基础模型声称的预处理不变性在很大程度上可能并非学习的结果，而是输入归一化层的固有属性。社区应改用能严格分离归一化作用的评估协议，避免将无关的预处理差异误报为模型习得的能力。

Abstract: Preprocessing invariance is an appealing goal for spectral foundation models: a frozen model should remain useful when laboratories preprocess spectra differently. It is usually measured by training a classifier under one preprocessing pipeline and testing it under another, with preserved accuracy read as evidence of learning. We revisit that reading, using a Raman foundation model as a case study. Such models normalize their inputs before any learned parameter is applied. If that normalization maps two differently preprocessed spectra to the same vector, the encoder receives identical inputs, so the invariance cannot be attributed to learning. For a normalization that uses each spectrum's own statistics, this happens exactly when one spectrum is a positive multiple of the other plus a constant. Several standard preprocessing operations take that form. The encoder should therefore be measured against the normalization alone, which has no learned parameters. On six Raman evaluation datasets, the model does not measurably outperform its own normalization. It improves on raw spectra, but so does the normalization alone. Training does improve the encoder over random initialization, and a controlled experiment shows that it learns to ignore a transformation only when that transformation reaches it. A numerical test settles which transformations a given normalization removes. Across released systems in five modalities, most normalizations already remove transformations of that form, and several of those systems claim that invariance as learned. Replicating the comparison on two of them shows no gain either.

</details>


### [113] [AI Evaluation Should Work With Humans](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13577)
*Jan Kulveit,Gavin Leech,Tomáš Gavenčiak,Raymond Douglas*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该立场论文主张AI评估范式应从"超人自主性能"转向"人机协作团队性能"，认为当前以替代人类为目标的评估方向是错误的，协作式评估将引导AI成为人类能力的真正补充，带来更好的社会结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文认为当前AI评估的主流范式（强调超人自主表现，隐含以替代人类为目标）正在将AI发展引向错误方向。作者希望纠正这一评估导向，使AI发展服务于增强而非取代人类能力。

Method: 这是一篇立场论文（position paper），通过论证方式提出观点：主张AI评估应从单一AI自主性能转向人机团队（human-AI team）性能的评估，以此作为新的评估范式。

Result: 论文指出协作式评估转变将促使AI系统成为人类能力的真正补充（true complements），从而产生比当前评估流程更优的社会结果。

Conclusion: 作者呼吁AI社区将评估重心从衡量AI取代人类的能力，转向衡量AI与人类协同工作的效能，以实现更好的社会发展方向。

Abstract: This position paper argues that the dominant paradigm of AI evaluation (which focuses on superhuman autonomous performance and so implicitly targets the goal of replacing humans) is guiding AI development in the wrong direction. Instead, the AI community should pivot to evaluating the performance of human--AI teams. We argue that this collaborative shift will foster AI systems that act as true complements to human capabilities and therefore lead to far better societal outcomes than will the current process.

</details>


### [114] [Stable Miscalibration in Large Language Models: A Practical View of High-Confidence Errors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13591)
*Akira Okutomi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究大型语言模型中"稳定误校准"现象——即高置信度的错误答案在微小扰动下仍保持局部稳定。作者结合输出层面的审计分数与内部隐蔽状态敏感性探测两种诊断方法，发现自我批评提示能通过提示诱导的局部稳定化降低隐蔽状态敏感性，但高置信错误并不比高置信正确回答更具局部敏感性，表明部分高置信错误可能是稳定且误校准的，而非简单的不稳定推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究常将LLM的高置信错误视为内部推理脆弱的证据，但作者提出另一种可能：某些错误答案在局部扰动下是稳定的（即"稳定误校准"），这挑战了"高置信错误必然源于不稳定的内部推理"这一传统假设，值得系统检验。

Method: 结合两种诊断方法：(1) 输出层面的审计分数——一种标签感知的输出级审计评分，在强制回答基线（forced-answer baseline）下对领域按置信度变化和过度自信错误排序；(2) 内部敏感性探测——测量隐蔽状态在扰动下的移动。在多领域二元事实审计集上对三个开放权重模型进行跨层分析。

Result: 审计分数能追踪"弃权感知的自我批评"（abstention-aware self-critique）在何处降低决策损失，但直接标记的基线对相同收益的排序更强。内部层面发现自我批评提示一致地降低三层开放权重模型所有层的隐蔽状态敏感性，支持提示诱导的局部稳定化而非单纯的输出层弃权模式。然而，审计定义的过度自信错误并不比高置信的正确回答更局部敏感，表明高置信错误的稳定性并非校准的证据。

Conclusion: 自我批评提示通过局部稳定化内部表征来减少高置信错误，但高置信错误本身可以是稳定且误校准的，而非仅仅归因于推理脆弱性。这提示在研究LLM置信度时应区分"局部稳定性"与"校准质量"两个不同维度。

Abstract: High-confidence errors in large language models are often treated as evidence of fragile internal inference. We study a different possibility: stable miscalibration, where a confident wrong answer remains locally stable under small perturbations. We combine two diagnostics: a label-aware output-level audit score that ranks domains by confidence variation and overconfident mistakes under a forced-answer baseline, and an internal sensitivity probe that measures hidden-state movement. On a multi-domain binary factual audit set, this audit score tracks where abstention-aware self-critique reduces decision loss, although direct labeled baselines rank the same gain more strongly. Internally, self-critical prompting consistently reduces hidden-state sensitivity across layers in three open-weight models. This supports prompt-induced local stabilization rather than a purely output-level abstention pattern, but it does not imply calibration: audit-defined overconfident errors are not clearly more locally sensitive than confidently correct answers, so some high-confidence errors may be stable and miscalibrated rather than simply fragile.

</details>


### [115] [Measuring Cross-Task Behavioral Consistency in Language Model Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13598)
*Amritesh Banerjee,Pranil Raichura*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了行为一致性度量（BCM），用于量化语言模型代理在执行软件工程任务时跨任务的行为一致性，区别于传统的结果指标（如成功率）。基于约9000条轨迹的实验表明，跨任务与任务内一致性是两个独立维度，且一致性无法简化为成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的代理评估几乎完全依赖成功率等结果指标，这些指标只能反映代理是否成功，无法捕捉其行为的一致性。作者认为行为一致性是一个独立且可度量的属性，值得系统研究。

Method: BCM通过训练一个模型从代理执行轨迹的行为特征中预测任务成功，生成每条轨迹的特征归因向量，然后在这些向量上计算系统内成对相似度的均值，从而量化一致性。

Result: 在六个语言模型代理、约9000条软件工程任务的轨迹上，发现跨任务一致性与任务内一致性是可分离的：有些系统局部可复现但全局碎片化，而另一些在两个尺度上都一致。同时发现一致性不可简化为成功率，且在前沿模型与开源模型之间的一致性差距在控制任务难度后仍然存在。

Conclusion: BCM可作为补充结果指标的过程级可靠性信号。作者也明确说明了该指标有意义的适用条件，将其定位为一种全新的代理评估维度。

Abstract: Agent evaluation relies almost entirely on outcome metrics such as success rate, which capture whether an agent succeeds but not how consistently it behaves. We argue that behavioral consistency across tasks is a distinct and measurable property, and we introduce the Behavioral Consistency Metric (BCM) to quantify it. BCM trains a model to predict task success from behavioral features of agent execution traces, derives a per-trajectory feature-attribution vector, and measures the mean pairwise similarity of these vectors within an agent system. Across roughly 9,000 trajectories from six language model agents on software engineering tasks, our central finding is that cross-task and within-task consistency are distinct axes that can diverge: some systems are locally reproducible, behaving similarly on repeated attempts at one task, yet globally fragmented, with no stable strategy across different tasks, while others are consistent at both scales. Prior work measures only same-task reproducibility and so cannot observe this separation. We further find that consistency is not reducible to success rate, since systems with comparable success can differ sharply in consistency, and that the frontier-versus-open-source consistency gap persists under a within-task control that holds task difficulty constant. We position BCM as a process-level reliability signal that complements outcome metrics, and we are explicit about the conditions under which it is meaningful.

</details>


### [116] [Cross-Disciplinary Taxonomy and Modeling of Misunderstanding Generation, Amplification, and Detection, from Pragmatics to AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13604)
*Babak Abbaschian*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的误解分类框架，将误解视为分层过程（分歧产生→放大→检测与修复），整合九个互不引用的研究领域的文献，识别出十一个精确失效模式并将其定位在沟通过程的八个分析层中，同时将信息与通信理论从信号传输扩展到意义重构，并提供了可审计的证据矩阵、编码手册和九个对话案例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着沟通从实时面对面的交互转向AI中介渠道，沟通者越来越无法依赖传统的修复资源来检测误解，而新的检测手段尚未跟上这一转变。因此，误解检测成为一个亟待解决的问题，需要更系统的理论框架来理解误解的产生机制和故障定位。

Method: 作者综合了九个通常不互相引用的学科领域的文献，识别出十一个具体失效模式（其中八个主要产生分歧，两个主要放大已有分歧，一个决定分歧是否被检测和修复）；根据各失效模式在沟通过程中的特定作用点，提取出八个分析层；对八个层进行形式化建模，将信息与通信理论从信号传输扩展到意义重构；提供逐来源的证据矩阵、编码手册和九个对话分析案例。

Result: 建立了基于过程位置和功能类型的双重维度的误解分类体系：十一个失效模式分布于八个分析层，每一模式都作用于沟通过程中的特定位置而非任意位置；八层模型通过扩展信息理论形式化了从信号到意义的重构过程；证据矩阵保障了评级的可审计性。

Conclusion: 该分类是首个同时满足"将机制定位于过程的具体点"和"按功能对机制进行分类"两个条件的误解分类体系，填补了现有误解检测研究缺乏系统定位框架的空白，为AI中介沟通场景下的误解检测提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Detection of misunderstanding is an urgent problem to solve because communication has moved away from real-time, in-person interaction and is increasingly handled by AI-mediated channels. This shift cuts communicators off from the resources repair depends on faster than new means of detection are being built. In this paper we analyse misunderstanding as a layered process in which a divergence is generated, may then be amplified, and is either detected and repaired or left to persist unnoticed. Consolidating accounts from nine fields of research that do not ordinarily cite one another, we identify eleven exact failure modes and show that each operates at a specific point in a communicative process rather than anywhere within it. Those points give eight analytical layers, derived from the literature rather than adopted from an existing model. Eight of the mechanisms primarily generate a divergence, two primarily amplify one already present, and one governs whether a divergence is detected and repaired. We model the eight layers formally, extending information and communication theory from the transmission of signals to the reconstruction of meaning, and we supply a source-by-source evidence matrix that makes every rating auditable, a coding manual, and nine analysed dialogue cases. No prior classification of misunderstanding both locates mechanisms at points in the process and types them by function.

</details>


### [117] [Active Perception for Embodied Disambiguation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13605)
*Yiwei Liu,Luwei Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个用于具身环境中目标消歧的主动感知框架，利用主动观测作为信息获取的核心手段，并结合视觉语言模型在持续观测、请求澄清或完成目标选择之间做决策，实现了物理信息获取与用户意图澄清的统一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在具身环境中，自然语言任务接口存在目标歧义问题，歧义不仅源于用户意图不明确，还可能源于当前观测中缺少与任务相关的物理证据（如遮挡、受限视角、不可读文字或未观察到的目标）。现有交互式消歧方法主要依赖向用户提问来获取额外信息，无法应对需要机器人主动改变观测视角的场景。

Method: 提出一个以主动观测为信息获取主干的具身目标消歧框架。使用视觉语言模型（VLM）基于累积的视觉证据和交互信息进行决策，判断当前状态是应该继续观测、请求用户澄清还是完成目标选择。主动观测既能直接恢复缺失的判别性物理证据，也能揭示物体名称、标签和语义属性，从而在仍需要澄清时提升用户澄清的质量。

Result: 真实机器人实验表明，该框架能够在统一的具身消歧过程中结合物理信息获取与用户意图澄清，验证了框架在真实场景下的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过将主动感知与视觉语言模型决策相结合，该框架可以统一物理证据获取和用户意图澄清，有效解决具身环境中的目标歧义问题，为机器人交互式消歧提供了新思路。

Abstract: Natural language provides robots with a flexible task interface, but target ambiguity in embodied environments arises not only from user intent; it can also result from missing taskrelevant physical evidence in the current observation. Existing interactive disambiguation methods primarily obtain additional information by asking the user, whereas occlusion, restricted viewpoints, unreadable text, and unobserved targets require the robot to actively change its observation. We propose an active-perception framework for embodied target disambiguation that uses active observation as the backbone for information acquisition and uses a vision-language model to decide, on the basis of accumulated visual evidence and interaction information, whether to continue observing, request clarification, or complete target selection. Active observation can both directly recover missing discriminative evidence and reveal object names, labels, and semantic attributes, thereby improving user clarification when it remains necessary.
