### [253] [Catching the Imposter: Self-Supervised Learning of Physical Coherence with Cross-Entity Feature Permutations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14372)
*Aleksei Rozanov,Arvind Renganathan,Vipin Kumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"imposter"自监督预训练任务，通过替换实体特征子集来迫使编码器学习跨特征的物理依赖关系，并在ERA5-Land再分析数据上对21个环境变量进行了系统评估，证明了物理一致性可作为科学基础模型的新型自监督信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学数据中实体的特征通常受物理定律的联合约束，但现有自监督学习目标大多忽略了这种物理一致性，导致学到的表示未能充分利用数据中蕴含的物理结构信息。

Method: 提出"imposter"判别式预文本任务：将某实体的一部分特征替换为另一实体贡献的真实观测值（每个替换值单独看都是合理的），训练编码器识别哪些特征被替换。由于无法通过单个特征的值来判断，模型必须学习跨特征的物理依赖关系才能解决该任务。在ERA5-Land数据上使用21个环境变量进行预训练，并与其他SSL目标在同一架构和预训练预算下进行系统比较。

Result: 在7个下游任务（气候分类、碳通量估计、径流预测）上的评估表明：最有效的预文本任务取决于下游任务族而非某个单一目标具有绝对优势；imposter与现有SSL目标组合时能提供互补信息。这也是首个在同一架构和预训练预算下对面向地表建模的自监督目标进行系统比较的研究。

Conclusion: 物理一致性是科学基础模型有价值的新型自监督信号来源，imposter任务能够有效捕捉跨特征物理依赖，并为现有SSL目标提供互补增益。

Abstract: Scientific data often describe entities whose features are jointly governed by the laws of physics, yet existing self-supervised learning (SSL) objectives largely ignore this physical coherence. We introduce imposter, a discriminative pretext task that replaces subsets of an entity's features with real observations donated by another entity and trains the encoder to identify the swapped features. Because every donated value is individually plausible, the task can only be solved by learning cross-feature physical dependencies. We evaluate the proposed objectives on global ERA5-Land reanalysis data using 21 environmental variables and assess the learned representations on seven downstream tasks spanning climate classification, carbon flux estimation, and streamflow prediction. Our study includes, to our knowledge, the first systematic comparison of self-supervised objectives for land-surface modeling under a shared architecture and pre-training budget. We find that the most effective pretext task depends on the downstream task family rather than any single objective's superiority, and that imposter provides complementary information when combined with existing SSL objectives. These results suggest that physical coherence is a valuable new source of self-supervision for scientific foundation models.

</details>


### [254] [Boosting Data Augmentation with Stochastic Weight Averaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14373)
*Longde Huang,Axel Flinth,Jan E. Gerken*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究随机权重平均（SWA）作为数据增强训练中深度集成的替代方案，通过在无限宽度极限下用Ornstein-Uhlenbeck过程近似训练轨迹，证明SWA在增强数据上能提供超出其本身性能提升的等变性增益，并在计算机视觉和图分类等众多模型中验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据增强是将学习任务的对称性融入通用神经网络的有效方式，但深度集成的完美对称性需要重复多次训练，成本高昂。需要一种不重复训练就能获得对称性增益的替代方法。

Method: 将SWA视为集成技术的替代方案，利用Ornstein-Uhlenbeck过程在训练末期近似随机训练轨迹，在无限宽度极限下从理论上分析SWA在增强数据上的等变性行为。

Result: 在无限宽度极限下，SWA结合数据增强提供的等变性提升超出了仅由SWA本身性能提升所能预期的大小，并通过涵盖计算机视觉和图分类（包括离散与连续对称性）的大量数值实验加以验证。

Conclusion: SWA无需重复训练即可在增强数据上获得超越其自身性能增益的等变性提升，是深度集成的一种实用且高效的替代方案。

Abstract: The symmetries of a learning task have become an important factor in designing modern deep learning solutions. Data augmentation is a straightforward and effective way of incorporating symmetries into a generic neural network. Recent results show that infinitely large deep ensembles show perfect symmetry when trained on augmented data. However, since training ensembles requires repeating the training process many times, this method is costly. In this work, we study stochastic weight averaging (SWA) as an alternative ensembling technique that does not require repeated training runs. We analyze SWA by approximating the stochastic training trajectory at the end of training with an Ornstein--Uhlenbeck process. We show that in the infinite-width limit, SWA on augmented data provides an equiviariance boost that goes beyond what could be expected from the performance increase due to SWA alone. We verify our results with extensive numerical experiments on numerous models spanning computer vision and graph classification with both discrete and continuous symmetries.

</details>


### [255] [DeaMoE: Efficient MoE Structure for Fast Small-Batch Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14385)
*Zewen Jin,Shen Fu,Zeping Duan,Shannon Wang,Weihao Wu,Chengjie Tang,Congkun Ai,Ping Gong,Zijian Dai,Youhui Bai,Cheng Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DeaMoE是一种面向解码的MoE架构，通过将专家按专业领域分组并使同组专家共享大部分参数、保留少量私有参数，配合定制化的两阶段路由策略，显著减少小批量解码下的权重加载量，最高可减少50.9%的每步加载权重，并实现最高2.00x的峰值加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE模型广泛用于实时交互应用（如编码助手、实时音视频交互），但小批量解码下推理受限于内存带宽，瓶颈在于专家权重的加载。现有解决方案（如训练后权重压缩、预训练阶段的细粒度专家设计）要么降低模型精度，要么引入额外的计算和通信开销。

Method: 提出DeaMoE架构：1）将专家分为若干"部门"（departments），同一部门的专家因来自同一专业领域而共享大部分参数，每个专家仅保留少量私有参数以体现其独特性；2）设计定制化的两阶段路由策略，避免冗余权重加载。

Result: 相比vanilla MoE，DeaMoE在7B预训练模型上减少了最高50.9%的每步加载权重，A40上实现最高1.33x的端到端TPOT加速；在DeepSeek-V3的微基准测试中，A40和H40上分别实现最高2.00x和1.97x的峰值加速。

Conclusion: DeaMoE通过参数共享的部门化专家设计与两阶段路由，有效缓解了小批量MoE解码时的内存带宽瓶颈，在不牺牲精度的前提下显著提升解码效率。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models have been widely adopted in real-time interactive applications such as coding assistants, real-time audio-video interaction systems. To meet the extremely low response latency requirements of these scenarios, practitioners commonly employ small-batch decoding, under which MoE inference becomes memory-bound and is severely bottlenecked by expert weight loading. However, this bottleneck has received limited attention, and existing solutions such as post-training weight compression or fine-grained expert design during pre-training either degrade model accuracy or introduce additional computation and communication overhead. To tackle this issue, we propose DeaMoE, a decoding-efficient MoE architecture, in which the experts are grouped into several departments, and the experts belonging to the same department share most parameters since they come from the same professional field, and additionally each expert contains a few private parameters to reflect its uniqueness. Moreover, we design customized two-stage routing strategy for DeaMoE to avoid redundant loading, under which DeaMoE greatly improves the efficiency during LLM decoding. Compared with vanilla MoE, DeaMoE reduces per-step loaded weights by up to 50.9% and achieves up to 1.33 end-to-end TPOT speedup for the pre-trained 7B model on A40, and up to 2.00x and 1.97x peak speedup for DeepSeek-V3 on A40 and H100 in microbenchmarks.

</details>


### [256] [CytoBERT: A Foundation Model for Cytometry Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14414)
*Syed Abdul Haseeb Qadri,Bjarne C. Hiller,Felix Blanke,Vanja Sophie Cangalovic,Kutalmış Coşkun,Amin Mirzaei,Tom Siegl,Sebastian Bader,Thomas Kirste,Martin Becker*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了CytoBERT，一个面向单细胞流式细胞术数据的开源基础模型。该模型在包含15个人类数据集、超过5000万个细胞的语料库上进行自监督预训练，通过标记标准化处理异构标记面板，实现了跨异质性数据集的迁移学习和样本级分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流式细胞术数据在免疫学和临床中广泛应用，但数据高度异质且缺乏标准化，导致机器学习方法难以跨研究迁移。尽管基础模型在其他领域取得了进展，但在细胞术领域仍缺乏相应方法，亟需一个能够处理可变标记面板的通用模型。

Method: CytoBERT采用自监督预训练方式，在大规模细胞术语料库（15个人类数据集、超过5000万细胞）上进行训练。通过标记标准化（marker standardization）处理异构标记面板，使模型学习细胞内可迁移的标记间关系。随后针对样本级分类任务进行微调。

Result: 在样本级分类微调任务中，CytoBERT证明了跨异质性细胞术数据集的迁移学习是可行的，为可扩展、可泛化的细胞术分析提供了基础。

Conclusion: CytoBERT作为首个开放源码的细胞术基础模型，展示了基础模型在单细胞流式细胞术领域的应用潜力，为处理标记面板异构问题提供了有效方案，为后续研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: Cytometry measures the complex characteristics of single cells (e.g., counts and protein expression of immune cells) and is widely used across immunological research and clinical settings. However, cytometry data is highly heterogeneous and unstandardized due to experimental protocols and the choice of measured features. While machine learning methods hold the potential to gain deeper insights into cell biology, these challenges make them difficult to apply and transfer across studies. Recent advances in foundation models can alleviate these issues, but corresponding approaches are still scarce in this field. To address this, we provide CytoBERT, a publicly available, open-source, open-weight foundation model for single-cell cytometry data with variable marker panels. CytoBERT is pretrained in a self-supervised manner on a large-scale cytometry corpus (15 human datasets with heterogeneous marker panels and more than 50 million cells) curated through marker standardization, enabling it to learn transferable inter-marker relationships within cells. Fine-tuning CytoBERT for sample-level classification demonstrates that transfer learning across heterogeneous cytometry datasets is feasible, providing a starting point for scalable, generalizable cytometry analysis. Code is available at GitHub.

</details>


### [257] [More Correct Mass, Worse Answers: Why Power Sampling Can Fail and How to Fix It](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14420)
*Haohui Yang,Jiaxing Sun,Xiujun Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现Power Sampling虽能锐化语言模型在完整生成轨迹上的分布进而提升推理能力，但存在一个悖论：它在将更多概率质量推向正确轨迹的同时，反而会降低下游推理性能（在自一致性任务上准确率下降最多18.5个百分点）。作者将成因归结为剂量失配和覆盖失配，并提出一种变形控制、支持保留的Power目标来修复该问题，在相同预算下优于全局Power Sampling和标准多样本推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Power Sampling作为一种无需验证器的推理时改进方法，有望作为通用前端服务于多种下游采样方法，但其实际应用中存在性能下降的悖论，需要系统诊断其成因并提出修复方案。

Method: 作者通过自一致性作为代表性案例，系统诊断出两个失配：（1）剂量失配——固定指数在不同问题间引发差异极大的分布变化量；（2）覆盖失配——全局锐化将质量集中在少数主导路径上，即便pass@k高也不代表保留了下游聚合所需的广泛推理路径支持。据此提出用变形控制且支持保留的Power目标替换均匀轨迹幂运算，对跨问题的锐化程度进行校准，同时限制中等概率路径的过度抑制。

Result: 在相同预算下，修复后的采样器结合加权自一致性，不仅逆转了全局Power Sampling造成的性能损失，还在多个推理基准上超越了标准多样本推理的性能。

Conclusion: Power Sampling的性能退化源于剂量与覆盖两方面的失配，而通过变形控制和支持保留的设计可以同时保证锐化效果与推理路径的多样性，使Power Sampling真正成为可靠的通用下游采样前端。

Abstract: Power Sampling sharpens a language model's distribution over complete generation trajectories, offering a verifier-free way to improve reasoning at inference time. It also has the potential to serve as a general-purpose front end for a broad range of downstream sampling methods. However, we uncover a striking paradox: Power Sampling can drive more probability mass toward correct trajectories while degrading the downstream inference it is intended to enhance. Using self-consistency as a representative case, we observe accuracy drops of up to 18.5 percentage points across models and reasoning benchmarks. We trace this paradox to two mismatches. Dose mismatch arises because a fixed exponent induces drastically different amounts of distributional change across problems. Coverage mismatch arises because global sharpening concentrates mass on a narrow set of dominant paths: high pass@k, often interpreted as evidence of preserved diversity, can therefore coexist with the loss of broad reasoning-path support required for downstream aggregation, search, and selection. Guided by this diagnosis, we replace uniform trajectory exponentiation with a deformation-controlled, support-preserving Power target that calibrates sharpening across problems while limiting the suppression of moderate-probability paths. In a same-budget instantiation with weighted self-consistency, the repaired sampler reverses the losses caused by global Power and outperforms standard multi-sample inference across reasoning benchmarks.

</details>


### [258] [Designing Reinforcement Learning for Diffusion Models: A Unified Path-Space View](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14430)
*Yixian Xu,Yuanrui Zhang,Shengjie Luo,Liwei Wang,Di He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明扩散模型的不同RL后训练方法（如Flow-GRPO、AWM、DiffusionNFT）实际上源自统一的路径空间原则，它们之间的经验差距本质上是方差缩减效应而非RL原则差异。以此为基础提出多样本KDE值梯度估计器和尺度有界权重族，在SD3.5-M和Qwen-Image上超越现有扩散RL基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前扩散模型的RL后训练算法呈现碎片化状态：反向轨迹方法依赖离散化似然比，前向匹配方法则在带奖励标签的噪声样本上训练。这些方法看似各不相同，缺乏统一的原理框架来理解和比较。

Method: 从正则化的扩散RL目标出发，利用采样SDE之间的重要性采样获得轨迹空间上的显式策略梯度估计器。该估计器包含Flow-GRPO类型更新中的随机Itô积分；进一步推导出等价的方差缩减值梯度形式，恢复了AWM和DiffusionNFT的前向匹配结构。基于该统一设计空间，提出多样本KDE值梯度估计器（重用rollout组）和尺度有界权重族。

Result: 在SD3.5-M和Qwen-Image模型上的实验验证了方差缩减解释的有效性，所提出的配方在多个扩散RL基线上取得改进。

Conclusion: 扩散RL方法族之间的差异本质上是方差缩减效果的差异，统一的路径空间原则为设计更优的扩散对齐算法提供了系统化的设计空间。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) post-training provides a direct way to align diffusion models with human preferences and task-specific rewards. However, current RL algorithms for diffusion models remain fragmented: reverse-trajectory methods rely on discretized likelihood ratios, whereas forward-matching methods train on reward-labeled noising versions of the rollout samples. This paper shows that these seemingly different losses arise from a single path-space principle. Starting from the regularized diffusion-RL objective, we use importance sampling between sampling SDEs to obtain an explicit policy-gradient estimator on trajectory space. The estimator contains the stochastic Itô integral underlying Flow-GRPO-type updates; we derive an equivalent variance-reduced value-gradient form that recovers the forward-matching structure of AWM and DiffusionNFT. This identifies the empirical gap between these method families as a variance-reduction effect rather than a difference in RL principle. The derivation yields a unified design space organized by value-gradient estimation, weight functions, and sampling choices. Within this space, we propose a multi-sample KDE value-gradient estimator that reuses rollout groups, together with scale-bounded weight families that retain stable existing recipes while excluding singular ones. Experiments on SD3.5-M and Qwen-Image models validate the variance-reduction explanation and show that the resulting recipe improves over prior diffusion-RL baselines.

</details>


### [259] [Designing Compact Neural Architectures via Neuron Gating and Mixed Activation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14443)
*Abhishek Shukla,Ankur Sinha,Faiz Hamid*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个通用的双层优化NAS框架，通过连续松弛替代离散的神经元和激活决策，衍生出三种可扩展的NAS方法（NAS-NG、NAS-MA、NAS-NGMA），在MLP、CNN、RNN和Transformer等多样架构上表现出色，能识别紧凑且预测性能强的架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NAS天然是一个双层优化问题，但由于离散架构决策、指数级增长的搜索空间以及训练候选架构的高昂成本，NAS在计算上非常昂贵。现有方法难以在多种架构类型中高效地找到紧凑且高性能的架构。

Method: 提出三种可扩展的连续松弛公式，分别替代离散的神经元级和激活级决策：(1) NAS-NG基于神经元门控；(2) NAS-MA基于混合激活；(3) NAS-NGMA结合神经元门控和混合激活。这些方法将组合架构空间转化为可微优化问题。

Result: 在MNIST和CIFAR-10上的MLP和CNN实验中，所提方法一致地识别出紧凑且预测性能竞争或更优的架构。MNIST上NAS-NGMA以7.69M MLP参数达到98.68%测试准确率，NAS-NG仅以0.26M CNN参数达到99.63%准确率；CIFAR-10上一致优于vanilla DARTS。此外，NAS-NG还能优化过度参数化及文献最优的架构，在减少参数的同时提高准确率。

Conclusion: 松弛双层优化被确立为离散NAS的可扩展替代方案，为高效的神经元级和激活级架构优化提供了通用框架，可应用于MLP、CNN、RNN和Transformer等多样架构。

Abstract: Neural Architecture Search (NAS) is naturally formulated as a bilevel optimization problem, where the upper-level optimizes the architecture using validation performance and the lower-level trains network parameters using training loss. However, NAS is computationally expensive due to discrete architectural decisions, exponentially growing search spaces, and the high cost of training candidate architectures. This work develops a general bilevel optimization framework for NAS across diverse architectures, including MLPs, CNNs, RNNs, and Transformers, to identify compact architectures with strong predictive performance. We propose three scalable formulations that replace discrete neuron- and activation-level decisions with continuous relaxations, enabling differentiable optimization over otherwise combinatorial architecture spaces. These formulations give rise to three NAS methods: NAS based on Neuron Gating (NAS-NG), NAS based on Mixed Activation (NAS-MA), and NAS based on Neuron Gating and Mixed Activation (NAS-NGMA). Experiments on MLPs and CNNs using MNIST and CIFAR-10 show that the proposed methods consistently identify compact architectures with competitive or improved predictive performance. On MNIST, NAS-NGMA achieves 98.68% test accuracy with 7.69M MLP parameters, while NAS-NG achieves 99.63% accuracy with only 0.26M CNN parameters. On CIFAR-10, the proposed methods consistently outperform vanilla DARTS. Further experiments demonstrate that NAS-NG can optimize substantially over-parameterized and literature-optimal architectures, improving accuracy while reducing parameters. These results establish relaxed bilevel optimization as a scalable alternative to discrete NAS and provide a general framework for efficient neuron- and activation-level architecture optimization.

</details>


### [260] [LP-NAS: Linear Programming-based Neural Architecture Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14472)
*Abhishek Shukla,Ankur Sinha,Faiz Hamid*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LP-NAS，一种基于线性规划的微分神经架构搜索框架。LP-NAS利用验证损失梯度和训练损失Hessian构建线性规划问题，计算同时保证泛化能力和模型参数最优性的架构更新方向，从而高效导航架构搜索空间。在DARTS搜索空间上衍生出S-LP-DARTS和R-LP-DARTS两种变体，在CIFAR-10/100和ImageNet上均优于标准DARTS及多个变体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微分NAS（如DARTS）通过将架构搜索空间松弛为连续域，使得连续优化原理可应用于NAS。然而，现有方法在架构优化过程中没有充分利用数学规划框架来处理最优性与泛化之间的权衡。作者旨在提出一个通用的数学规划框架，以更高效、更有效地指导架构搜索。

Method: LP-NAS将架构搜索形式化为线性规划问题：利用验证损失梯度捕捉泛化信息，利用训练损失Hessian捕捉参数最优性约束。通过求解该LP获得架构更新方向，沿该方向进行梯度下降式更新。提出两种计算高效变体：S-LP-NAS（简化版本）和R-LP-NAS（鲁棒版本），分别应用到DARTS搜索空间形成S-LP-DARTS和R-LP-DARTS。

Result: 在CIFAR-10和CIFAR-100上，LP-DARTS在架构搜索和评估阶段均优于标准DARTS。在CIFAR-10上与P-DARTS、PC-DARTS、STO-DARTS等变体对比表现出有效性。搜索前期收敛更快，验证性能显著更高；在ImageNet上验证了所发现架构的可迁移性。

Conclusion: 基于线性规划的NAS框架能够有效结合泛化性和参数最优性双重约束，在保持计算效率的同时实现更快的收敛和更高的验证性能。LP-NAS是一个可适用于广泛连续搜索空间的通用数学规划框架，具有良好的可扩展性和迁移性。

Abstract: Neural Architecture Search (NAS) aims to automate neural network architecture design, reducing reliance on human expertise. Among the various NAS methods, differentiable NAS has gained prominence due to its efficiency and accuracy compared to conventional NAS approaches. Since differentiable NAS relaxes the architecture search space into a continuous domain, it is possible to apply principles from continuous optimization to NAS. In this paper, we propose Linear Programming-based NAS (LP-NAS), a mathematical programming-based framework for differentiable NAS that is applicable to a wide range of continuous search spaces. LP-NAS formulates a linear program (LP) using the validation-loss gradient and the training-loss Hessian to compute an architecture update direction that improves generalization while preserving the optimality of the model parameters. By following this LP-derived descent direction, LP-NAS efficiently navigates the architecture search space, leading to faster and more effective architecture optimization. We introduce two computationally efficient variants of LP-NAS, namely S-LP-NAS and R-LP-NAS. Applying LP-NAS to the Differentiable Architecture Search (DARTS) search space results in two algorithmic variants, S-LP-DARTS and R-LP-DARTS. Both variants achieve faster convergence and significantly higher validation performance during the early search iterations than the standard DARTS algorithm. Extensive experiments on CIFAR-10 and CIFAR-100 show that LP-DARTS outperforms standard DARTS in both the architecture search and evaluation phases. Additionally, we compare our approach with several DARTS variants (P-DARTS, PC-DARTS, and STO-DARTS) on the CIFAR-10 dataset and demonstrate its effectiveness. Furthermore, we validate the transferability of the discovered architectures through experiments on the ImageNet dataset.

</details>


### [261] [Approximate Muon with low-rank adapters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14492)
*Ben Anson,Conor Houghton,Edward Milsom*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出sMuon方法，通过线性化和最小二乘法近似低秩设定下的Muon优化器目标，使得Muon优化器能够与LoRA等参数高效微调方法自然结合，实验表明在SFT和ReLoRA预训练中取得了适度的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Muon优化器在神经网络预训练中表现优于其他优化器，但在参数高效微调（PEFT）中较少使用。根本原因是LoRA的低秩参数化在数学上无法直接对权重更新进行正交化，导致Muon无法与最常用的LoRA方法自然结合。

Method: 通过线性化将Muon目标函数松弛到低秩设定下，并利用最小二乘法近似求解，设计了一个仅使用矩阵乘法运算的高效实现（sMuon），避免了更复杂的线性代数分解操作。

Result: sMuon在SFT和ReLoRA预训练实验中表现良好。尽管结果因模型和评估方式而异，但总体上使用Muon进行低秩微调提供了适度的性能改进。

Conclusion: sMuon成功解决了Muon优化器与LoRA低秩微调结合的问题，为低秩微调场景下利用Muon优化器提供了可行方案，并带来了适度的性能提升。

Abstract: The Muon optimizer shows clear benefits versus alternatives when pretraining neural networks. However, it is used less frequently for parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT). One potential reason is that the most common PEFT method, LoRA, does not naturally combine with Muon since it is not mathematically possible to orthogonalize the weight update given by a low-rank parameterization. In this paper, we address this issue by approximating the solution to a relaxed Muon objective in the low-rank setting via linearization and then least-squares. We provide an efficient implementation that uses matmul operations only, as opposed to more complex linear algebra decomposition routines. Our method, sMuon (small Muon), performs favourably across SFT and a ReLoRA pretraining experiment. While results are model- and eval-dependent, we find overall that using Muon for low-rank fine-tuning provides moderate performance improvements.

</details>


### [262] [Generating Benchmark Health Data Using a Tabular Diffusion Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14496)
*Hao Yan,Lisa Pilgram,Dan Liu,Linglong Kong,Fida Dankar,Khaled El Emam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一个两阶段框架用于跨表格数据生成（CTDG），第一阶段将异构原始表格转换为统一的统计表格，第二阶段用扩散Transformer生成合成统计表格，再重建为原始异构表格，支持从多个异构表格中学习统一生成模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有合成表格数据生成方法主要局限于单输入表场景，难以有效处理具有多样特征集的多个异构表格，缺乏跨表格数据生成的能力。

Method: 两阶段框架：(1) 第一阶段：将每个异构原始表格转换为具有相同列集合的标准化统计表格，每个统计表格捕获原始列的边际分布和两两相关性；(2) 第二阶段：训练扩散Transformer模型学习同质统计表格间的结构模式并生成合成统计表格，再通过多元高斯采样和逆概率积分变换重建合成原始表格。

Result: 实验结果表明该方法在学习到的统计表示上具有高保真度，在生成的合成数据中具有良好的保真度-多样性权衡，验证了该方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 该两阶段CTDG框架能够从多个异构表格中学习统一生成模型，并支持生成无限数量的逼真合成异构表格，弥补了现有方法在跨表格生成方面的不足。

Abstract: Cross-Tabular Data Generation (CTDG) seeks to learn a generative model from multiple heterogeneous tables and produce new synthetic tabular datasets. However, existing synthetic tabular data generation methods are largely restricted to single-input-table scenarios and struggle to effectively handle multiple heterogeneous tables with diverse feature sets. To address this limitation, we propose a two-stage framework for cross-tabular data generation. In the first stage, each heterogeneous raw table is transformed into a standardized statistical table with the same set of columns across all tables. Each statistical table captures the marginal distributions of the original columns and the pairwise correlations among them. In the second stage, a diffusion transformer model is trained to capture structural patterns across these homogeneous statistical tables and to generate synthetic statistical tables. Synthetic raw tables are subsequently reconstructed from the generated statistical tables via multivariate Gaussian sampling followed by an inverse probability integral transform. This two-stage CTDG framework enables the learning of a unified generative model from multiple heterogeneous tables and supports the generation of an unlimited number of realistic synthetic heterogeneous tables. Experimental results demonstrate high fidelity in the learned statistical representations and a favorable fidelity-diversity trade-off in the generated synthetic data, validating the effectiveness of the proposed approach.

</details>


### [263] [Rollplex: Cross-Phase GPU Spatial Sharing for Vision Language Model Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14498)
*Hanfeng Lu,Tianyu Feng,Suyi Li,Yuheng Zhao,Wei Gao,Shaopan Xiong,Ju Huang,Siran Yang,Jiamang Wang,Lin Qu,Wei Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Rollplex，一种针对视觉语言模型（VLM）强化学习后训练的运行时间系统，通过将参考评分和训练阶段中的前缀计算移入rollout解码窗口，实现阶段重叠执行，在32块H800 GPU上获得1.23倍到2.24倍的加速，同时保持同步on-policy RL语义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有on-policy RL运行时对VLM而言存在严重的GPU利用不足问题。传统RL运行时将rollout、参考评分和actor训练串行执行，这种阶段粒度执行在VLM场景下尤其浪费，因为处理密集视频输入和提示前缀在各阶段占据大量时间，而这些前缀计算由于独立于生成响应，完全可以在rollout解码时并行执行。

Method: Rollplex包含两个核心机制：（1）阶段感知的内存管理，根据生产者-消费者生命周期控制HBM驻留；（2）并行度感知的权重共享，对不同TP度下布局兼容的张量使用相同的物理存储，仅重建不兼容的张量，避免完整的第二份actor副本。整体上，Rollplex将参考和训练阶段分解，并将前缀计算调入rollout解码窗口，实现阶段重叠。

Result: 在32块H800 GPU上，Rollplex相比串行共置获得1.23倍到1.30倍的加速，相比在相同GPU预算下的分散部署（disaggregation）获得1.57倍到2.24倍的加速，同时保持同步RL更新语义。

Conclusion: 通过将前缀计算与rollout解码重叠、并利用阶段感知内存管理和并行度感知权重共享解决显存约束，Rollplex有效提升了VLM RL后训练的资源利用率，在不破坏on-policy同步语义的前提下显著缩短训练时间。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) enable embodied agents to reason and act from visual observations and language instructions. Reinforcement learning (RL) post-training enhances these capabilities using task feedback, but current on-policy RL runtimes execute rollout, reference scoring, and actor training in strict serial phases. While effective for text-only RL, this phase-granular execution is wasteful for VLMs, where processing dense video inputs and prompt prefixes occupies a large fraction of each phase. Because prefix processing is independent of the generated response, it can be run alongside rollout decoding, which leaves GPU compute capacity underutilized, without breaking synchronous on-policy semantics.
  We present Rollplex, a runtime that decomposes the reference and training phase and moves the prefix computation into the rollout decode window. Realizing this schedule requires more than concurrent kernel launches: naive colocation of Qwen2.5-VL-32\,B requires roughly 165\,GiB per GPU, while rollout and training prefer different tensor-parallel (TP) degrees and weight layouts. Rollplex addresses these constraints with two mechanisms. Phase-aware memory management controls HBM residency according to producer--consumer lifetimes. Parallelism-aware weight sharing uses the same physical storage for layout-compatible tensors across distinct TP degrees and reconstructs only incompatible tensors, avoiding a complete second actor copy. On 32 H800 GPUs, Rollplex achieves $1.23\times$--$1.30\times$ speedup over serial colocation and $1.57\times$--$2.24\times$ over disaggregation under the same GPU budget, while preserving the synchronous RL update.

</details>


### [264] [RecipeNet: A Hierarchical Transformer for Recipe Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14505)
*Pin-Yen Huang,Sachin Chhabra,Prasanth Sai Gouripeddi,Abhinav Kumar,Baoxin Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RecipeNet是一个针对配方（recipe）数据的分层Transformer架构，通过堆叠的Transformer编码器分别在步骤内部建模字段级交互、在步骤之间建模序列依赖，在多个配方数据集和任务上优于现有表格学习模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 配方数据（如材料合成、药物配方、工业制造）以有序步骤序列形式呈现，包含异构结构化字段。现有表格学习方法通常将此类结构扁平化为固定模式表示，无法捕获层次化的字段交互和过程依赖性。

Method: RecipeNet采用分层Transformer架构：底层Transformer编码器负责编码每个步骤内部各字段之间的交互，上层Transformer编码器负责建模步骤之间的顺序依赖关系，通过堆叠的方式实现层次化表示学习。

Result: 在多个配方数据集和任务上的实验表明，RecipeNet一致性地优于现有表格学习模型，验证了层次化与序列化建模对配方表示学习的有效性。

Conclusion: 分层和序列建模对于配方数据的表示学习具有重要意义，RecipeNet为包含有序步骤和异构字段的结构化过程数据提供了一种有效的建模范式。

Abstract: Recipe data arises in domains such as materials synthesis, pharmaceutical formulation, and industrial manufacturing, where procedures are represented as ordered sequences of steps containing heterogeneous structured fields. Existing tabular learning methods typically flatten this structure into fixed-schema representations, limiting their ability to capture hierarchical field interactions and procedural dependencies. We propose RecipeNet, a hierarchical Transformer architecture that encodes field-level interactions within each step and sequential dependencies across steps through stacked Transformer encoders. Experiments on multiple recipe datasets and tasks demonstrate that RecipeNet consistently outperforms existing tabular models, highlighting the value of hierarchical and sequential modeling for recipe representation learning.

</details>


<div id='cs.CE'></div>

# cs.CE [[Back]](#toc)

### [265] [AlphaSeek: Trajectory-Level Self-Iterative Factor Mining Framework for Multi-Source Financial Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13913)
*Qilu Zhu,Zijun Lu,Jianmin Zhu,Ning Chen,Shuo Yin,Simon Fong*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: AlphaSeek是一个端到端的量化投资因子挖掘框架，整合了自动化方向发现、轨迹级因子演化挖掘和自我迭代组合优化，在CSI300上实现了ARR 8.28%、IR 1.29、MDD 6.28%的策略表现，且因子在CSI500上展现出良好的零样本跨市场迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM驱动的量化因子挖掘方法存在主观方向设计、多源信息整合不足、语义漂移、因子冗余以及缺乏从因子发现到组合回测的端到端反馈回路等局限性。

Method: AlphaSeek首先收集并总结多源金融信息以识别有潜力的挖掘方向；然后通过将优化单元从单一因子表达式扩展为完整研究轨迹（涵盖假设生成、因子构建、验证、回测与反馈），实现轨迹级因子挖掘；引入并行方向扩展、突变和交叉等演化算子提升搜索多样性、优化质量与因子鲁棒性；最后构建自迭代因子组合，使新发现因子与现有SOTA因子库在冗余感知约束下交互。

Result: 在CSI300上达到最优策略级表现：ARR为8.28%，IR为1.29，MDD为6.28%；因子预测指标IC为0.0454；CSI300上挖掘的因子在CSI500上同样表现出强时间序列收益，展示出零样本设置下良好的跨市场迁移性。

Conclusion: AlphaSeek通过端到端的框架设计和轨迹级演化机制，有效解决了现有因子挖掘方法的多个关键缺陷，在策略表现和因子预测能力上均取得有竞争力的结果，并验证了跨市场迁移的可行性。

Abstract: With the rapid rise of large language models, LLM-driven quantitative factor mining has become an increasingly active research area. However, existing methods still suffer from subjective direction design, limited integration of up-to-date multi-source information, semantic drift, factor redundancy, and the absence of an end-to-end feedback loop from factor discovery to portfolio backtesting. To address these limitations, we propose AlphaSeek, an end-to-end factor mining framework for quantitative investment that integrates automated direction discovery, trajectory-level factor evolution mining and self-iterative portfolio optimization. AlphaSeek first collects and summarizes multi-source financial information to identify promising mining directions. It then performs trajectory-level factor mining by extending the optimization unit from a single factor expression to a complete research trajectory covering hypothesis generation, factor construction, validation, backtesting, and feedback. Based on this design, we introduce evolution operators - parallel direction expansion, mutation and crossover - to improve search diversity, refinement quality and factor robustness. Finally, AlphaSeek constructs a self-iterative factor portfolio, allowing newly discovered factors to interact with an existing state-of-the-art(SOTA) factor library under redundancy-aware constraints. Experiments on CSI300 show that AlphaSeek achieves the strongest overall strategy-level performance on CSI300 with ARR of 8.28%, IR of 1.29 and MDD of 6.28%, while remaining competitive on factor predictive metrics with IC of 0.0454, while factors mined on CSI300 also achieve strong time-series return performance on CSI500 than other models, suggesting promising cross-market transferability under a zero-shot setting.

</details>


### [266] [MMDynOpt-Agent: Dynamic Optimization for Multimodal Large Language Model Reasoning via Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14026)
*Wenjin Liu,Haoran Luo,Fayuan Ke,Zhenghong Lin,Yue Lu,Zhe Cui,Anh Tuan Luu,Carl Yang*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 本文提出MMDynOpt-Agent，通过端到端强化学习将多模态推理的动态优化建模为马尔可夫决策过程，利用轻量级多模态智能体作为决策策略，与目标MLLM环境交互，通过多轮动态优化提示自适应引导推理，并设计结合格式合规、答案正确性和预算感知的奖励机制，在15个公开数据集上取得优异表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）在视觉理解和复杂推理任务中展现出潜力，但现有方法难以高效地将多模态输入的视觉线索和问题语义转化为有效的推理条件，从而限制了MLLMs的推理性能。

Method: 将多模态推理的动态优化建模为一个马尔可夫决策过程（MDP），通过端到端强化学习训练。使用一个轻量级多模态智能体作为决策策略，与目标MLLM（作为环境）交互，通过多轮动态优化提示自适应引导推理方向；同时设计融合格式合规、答案正确性和预算感知的奖励机制，兼顾推理准确性和效率。模型具有可迁移性和泛化性，可用一个目标MLLM训练并推理时迁移到其他MLLM。

Result: 在15个公开数据集上的实验表明，MMDynOpt-Agent取得了强性能，并且优于基线方法。

Conclusion: MMDynOpt-Agent通过强化学习驱动动态多模态推理优化，有效提升了多模态大语言模型的推理能力，同时兼顾效率，且具备跨模型的迁移泛化能力。

Abstract: Recently, multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have demonstrated strong potential in visual understanding and complex reasoning tasks. However, existing methods often struggle to efficiently transform visual cues from multimodal inputs and the semantics of the question into effective reasoning conditions, thereby limiting the reasoning performance of multimodal large language models. To address this challenge, we propose MMDynOpt-Agent, which models the dynamic optimization of multimodal reasoning as a Markov decision process via end-to-end reinforcement learning. Specifically, a lightweight multimodal agent serves as the decision policy and interacts with the target MLLM as the environment, adaptively steering its reasoning through multi-turn dynamic optimization prompts. Furthermore, to reduce the cost of multimodal reasoning, a reward mechanism that combines format compliance, answer correctness, and budget awareness is designed to jointly ensure reasoning accuracy and efficiency. MMDynOpt-Agent is transferable and generalizable, enabling training with one target MLLM and inference-time transfer to others. Experimental results on fifteen public datasets show MMDynOpt-Agent achieves strong performance and outperforms baselines. Our project is available at https://github.com/QwenQKing/MMDynOpt-Agent.

</details>


<div id='cs.IT'></div>

# cs.IT [[Back]](#toc)

### [267] [SN-ASMO: Satellite-Navigation Array Spatial-Manifold Precise Observation Theory A Mathematical Foundation for Observation Formation, Unified U(1) Geometry, Intrinsic Information, and Preservation of the RTK Integer Structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13611)
*Xianwei Meng*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出SN-ASMO框架，统一解决卫星导航中抑制干扰、高动态平台运动与载波相位RTK定位之间的结构耦合问题，确保接收机主动参与观测形成时载波观测仍保持连续相位和整数模糊度结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当接收机通过阵列几何、信道状态、权重和信号处理规则主动参与观测形成时，如何保证载波观测仍代表同一客观传播过程并保持连续相位和整数模糊度结构？若无传播因素与观测者因素变异的严格分离，抗干扰、高动态和精密载波相位定位将在观测形成层面产生结构耦合。

Method: SN-ASMO框架统一了观测形成、参考协方差、精确噪声商、同源传输、内禀统计信息，并强调载波RTK整数结构的保持，提供了一个数学闭合且物理可检验的统一框架。同时给出了静态干扰、动态旋转、支路间定时失配、近零响应、射频回放和RTK整数风险验证等可复现可证伪的协议。

Result: 该框架通过严格分离传播诱导变异与观测者诱导变异，解决了抑制干扰、高动态运动与精密载波相位定位在观测形成层面的结构性耦合问题，并提供了可复现、可证伪的验证协议。

Conclusion: SN-ASMO的标新之处在于将观测形成、参考协方差、精确噪声商、同源传输、内禀统计信息与RTK整数结构保持统一于一个数学上闭合、物理上可测试的框架内，为该领域的观测形成问题提供了系统化解决方案。

Abstract: Suppressive interference, high-dynamic motion of the receiving platform, and carrier-phase RTK lead satellite navigation to one fundamental question: when the receiver actively participates in observation formation through its array geometry, channel states, weights, and signal-processing rules, how can the resulting carrier observation continue to represent the same objective propagation process while preserving continuous phase and the integer-ambiguity structure? The physical world is not changed by a reconfiguration of the observer, but its complex representation in the observation world is. Without a strict separation between propagation-induced and observer-induced variations, anti-jamming, high dynamics, and precise carrier-phase positioning become structurally coupled at the observation-formation level.
  Finally, reproducible and falsifiable protocols are provided for static interference, dynamic rotation, inter-branch timing mismatch, near-null response, RF replay, and RTK integer-risk validation. The scoped novelty of SN-ASMO is the unification of observation formation, reference covariance, exact nuisance quotients, same-source transport, intrinsic statistical information, and preservation of the RTK integer structure in one mathematically closed and physically testable framework.

</details>


### [268] [A Survey of Typical-Cell Volume Distributions in Poisson--Voronoi and Poisson--Delaunay Tessellations: Analytical Theory, High-Dimensional Limits, and Wireless Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13615)
*Minghua Xia,Tian Shi,Wenkunn Wen*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文系统综述了泊松-沃罗诺伊与泊松-德劳内镶嵌中典型单元体积分布的研究进展，涵盖解析方法、精确与近似结果、高维极限以及无线网络应用，并指出了若干开放问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 泊松-沃罗诺伊与泊松-德劳内镶嵌作为随机几何中的典型对偶模型，其典型单元体积分布对覆盖性、流量负载、聚类、连通性等系统特性建模至关重要。然而文献中存在明显的分析不对称性：德劳内单纯形体积已有维度显式的PDF、CDF和矩公式，而沃罗诺伊单元体积仍缺乏可解析处理的闭式分布，实际建模多依赖模拟和数值近似。这种不对称性促使作者对这一领域进行系统综述。

Method: 本文采用综述方法，系统回顾两类镶嵌中典型单元体积分布的解析工具与成果：对于泊松-沃罗诺伊，回顾平面情形的精确积分表示、近期提出的尺度-形状因子分解（条件Gamma结构）；对于泊松-德劳内，梳理基于Mellin变换分析和Meijer G函数表示的显式解析公式，并综合高维极限结果与无线网络应用。

Result: 论文整合了泊松-沃罗诺伊体积分布的已知精确与近似结果（包括条件Gamma结构、数值近似与矩表征），以及泊松-德劳内单纯形体积的维度显式闭式公式；总结了高维极限行为，并展示了这些分布在无线网络负载建模、协作传输和三维架构分析中的应用。

Conclusion: 作者指出泊松-沃罗诺伊单元体积的无条件闭式分布仍是核心开放问题，同时提出非泊松空间模型、数据驱动几何推断以及维度感知网络建模等未来研究方向，强调需要弥合两类镶嵌在分析可处理性上的差距。

Abstract: Random spatial tessellations generated by point processes provide fundamental models for proximity, space partitioning, and local geometry in stochastic systems. Poisson--Voronoi and Poisson--Delaunay tessellations induced by homogeneous Poisson point processes form a canonical dual pair used in stochastic geometry, computational geometry, spatial statistics, and wireless-network analysis. Their typical-cell volume distributions provide important geometric inputs for modeling coverage, traffic load, clustering, connectivity, and other system characteristics. Despite extensive study, the literature remains analytically asymmetric. For Poisson--Voronoi cell volumes, exact integral representations exist in certain planar settings, while a recent scale--shape factorization provides an exact general-dimensional representation with conditional Gamma structure. However, the normalized shape laws and unbounded facet-count mixture remain implicit, and tractable unconditional closed-form distributions are unavailable. Practical modeling therefore relies largely on simulation, moment characterizations, and empirical approximations. By contrast, Poisson--Delaunay simplex volumes admit dimension-explicit PDFs, CDFs, and moment formulas derived through Mellin-transform analysis and Meijer's \(G\)-function representations. Motivated by this contrast, this paper surveys typical-cell volume distributions in Poisson--Voronoi and Poisson--Delaunay tessellations. We review the main analytical methods, synthesize exact and approximate results, summarize emerging high-dimensional limits, and discuss wireless-network applications, including load modeling, cooperative transmission, and three-dimensional architectures. We also identify open problems concerning unconditional Poisson--Voronoi distributions, non-Poisson spatial models, data-driven geometric inference, and dimension-aware network modeling.

</details>


### [269] [Energy-Aware Compression-Computation Co-Adaptation for Latency Minimization in Multi-User Semantic Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13632)
*Loc X. Nguyen,Yumin Park,Avi Deb Raha,Huy Q. Le,Zhu Han,Eui-Nam Huh,Choong Seon Hong*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文针对深度联合信源信道编码(DeepJSCC)语义通信系统中用户异构性问题（信道质量、QoS预期、本地能量差异），提出了一种能量感知的压缩-计算协同自适应(CoCo)框架。该框架允许基站通过调整信号长度或任务卸载来满足用户QoS，同时用户需消耗能量对信号去噪。通过将问题分解为参数优化和资源分配两个子问题，提出无需重训练的鲁棒编解码器和贪心子载波分配算法，在AWGN信道上验证了CoCo相比速率自适应或仅去噪方案能降低总时延并满足个体用户需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DeepJSCC语义通信工作难以适配用户异构性——不同用户在通信信道质量、期望QoS目标、可用本地能量三方面存在显著差异，而当前方案缺乏对这些异构性的显式建模和协同优化。

Method: 1) 显式建模用户异构性（QoS差异、信道条件、本地能量），数学化定义优化问题；2) 提出CoCo（压缩-计算协同自适应）框架，基站可发射更长信号或卸载任务至本地，用户消耗能量进行信号去噪以恢复高保真潜在特征；3) 将原问题分解为参数优化与资源分配两个子问题；4) 设计鲁棒编解码器，能在多种压缩率和信道噪声下无需重训练工作；5) 采用贪心子载波分配算法降低通信时间。

Result: 在标准图像数据集上的AWGN信道仿真结果表明，CoCo相比仅速率自适应的DeepJSCC或仅去噪方案，能够降低总时延，同时确保每个个体用户的QoS需求得到满足。

Conclusion: CoCo框架通过压缩率与计算量的联合自适应，有效应对用户异构性，在满足多样化用户需求的同时提升时延性能，为异构无线网络中的语义通信提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Deep joint source-channel coding-enabled (DeepJSCC) semantic communication (SemCom) has excelled at delivering high perceptual quality at low channel-bandwidth ratios, which positions it as a pillar for next-generation wireless networks. However, the existing works have difficulty accommodating user heterogeneity in terms of communication channel quality, expected quality-of-service (QoS) targets, and the available local energy. Therefore, in this paper, we explicitly reflect the heterogeneity of user devices in terms of the differences in expected QoS, channel condition, and local energy, and then mathematically formulate the problem. Next, we propose an energy-aware compression-computation co-adaptation (CoCo) framework, in which the base station can meet the expected user QoS by transmitting a longer signal or offloading the task to a local device. The user has to dedicate energy to denoising the signal to recover higher-fidelity latent features before feeding it to the semantic decoder. To solve the formulated problem, we first decompose it into two sub-problems: parameter optimization and resource allocation problems. Specifically, we propose a robust codec that effectively works under a diversity of compression rates and channel noise without re-training, while the greedy sub-carrier allocation lowers the communication time. Finally, we present simulation results on standard image datasets over additive white Gaussian noise to demonstrate the effectiveness of CoCo, which reduces total latency relative to rate-only adaptive DeepJSCC or denoising-only, thereby ensuring the demands of each individual user are met.

</details>


### [270] [Optimal Power Allocation and AI Receiver Design for Superimposed DMRS and Data Transmission](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13809)
*Sha Hu,Zhongwang Fu*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究了OFDM MIMO系统中叠加DMRS与数据的传输方案，提出了分析框架以刻画迭代信道估计与检测（ICED）过程中MSE的迭代行为，并在此基础上优化功率分配与导频图案；同时设计了基于Transformer编码器的AI接收机，集成ICED结构。仿真表明所提AI-ICED接收机结合叠加DMRS相比传统非重叠方案能有效提升频谱效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统OFDM MIMO系统中DMRS与数据符号在时频资源上非重叠分配，导频开销占用额外资源，限制了频谱效率。通过将DMRS叠加在数据符号上进行传输，可以消除导频开销，从而提升频谱效率。但叠加传输会引入导频与数据之间的干扰，需要设计有效的迭代接收机及优化方案来应对这一挑战。

Method: 1) 建立了分析框架，刻画ICED过程中信道估计MSE与MIMO检测MSE之间的迭代演化行为；2) 基于该框架优化DMRS与数据符号之间的功率分配及导频图案；3) 设计了基于Transformer编码器的AI接收机，内置ICED结构，用于SI-DMRS传输。

Result: 仿真结果表明，所提出的AI-ICED接收机结合SI-DMRS传输方案相比传统非重叠DMRS与数据符号的系统，能够有效提升频谱效率。

Conclusion: 叠加DMRS与数据的传输方案配合AI-ICED接收机是提升OFDM MIMO系统频谱效率的有效途径，其中分析框架为功率分配和导频图案优化提供了理论指导，而Transformer编码器则提供了强大的接收端信号处理能力。

Abstract: In this paper, we consider transmissions with superimposed (SI) demodulation-reference-symbol (DMRS) and data in orthogonal frequency-division multiplexing (OFDM) based multiple-input multiple-output (MIMO) systems. First, we derive an analytical framework to characterize the iterative behavior between the mean-square errors (MSEs) of channel estimation (CE) and MIMO detection (MD) within an iterative CE and detection (ICED) process. This framework is subsequently utilized to optimize power allocation and pilot patterns between the DMRS and data symbols for SI-DMRS transmission. Second, we design an artificial intelligence (AI) based receiver built upon Transformer encoders for SI-DMRS transmissions, which incorporates an iterative CE and detection (ICED) structure. Simulation results demonstrate that the proposed AI-ICED receiver, combined with SI-DMRS, effectively increases spectral efficiency (SE) compared to conventional systems using non-overlapped DMRS and data symbols.

</details>


### [271] [The Capacity Region of the Multiple Access Channel with Non-Signaling Assistance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13860)
*Yuhang Yao,Syed A. Jafar*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文完整刻画了K发送者离散无记忆多址接入信道（MAC）在非信号（NS）辅助下的容量区域，证明了其与经典容量区域形式相同，仅允许输入分布在发送者间任意依赖。并证明NS辅助的容量增益最大值为K。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前Fawzi和Fermé仅对K=2发送者的MAC建立了NS辅助的外界，且NS辅助MAC容量区域的一般（K发送者）情况尚未得到完整刻画。本文旨在将该结果推广到任意K个发送者，并探讨NS辅助带来的容量增益的上界。

Method: 本文通过将NS辅助下的MAC容量区域刻画为与经典容量区域同构的形式（仅放宽输入分布的独立性约束），将Fawzi和Fermé的K=2外边界自然推广到K个发送者。同时构造具体的K发送者MAC实例，证明NS辅助的乘法增益可任意接近K，并结合已有上界确定K为极值。

Result: 证明了K发送者NS辅助MAC的容量区域具有与经典容量区域相同的形式，仅允许发送者间输入分布任意依赖；给出了K发送者MAC实例，其中NS辅助的容量乘法增益可任意接近K，并结合此前上界确立了K为所有K发送者MAC设置中NS辅助乘法增益的极值。

Conclusion: 本文完整解决了K发送者MAC中NS辅助容量区域刻画问题，并确定了NS辅助容量增益的极值，为多用户信息理论中非信号辅助通信的研究提供了重要的理论完善。

Abstract: The capacity region of the $K$-sender discrete memoryless multiple access channel (MAC) is fully characterized when non-signaling (NS) assistance is available to all $K$ transmitters and the receiver. It is shown to have the same form as the classical capacity region of the MAC, except that the input distribution is allowed to be arbitrarily dependent across the senders. In particular, the NS-assisted capacity region matches the natural generalization to $K$ senders of an outer bound that was previously established by Fawzi and Fermé for $K=2$ senders. Additionally, we provide examples of $K$-sender MACs where the multiplicative gain in capacity from NS-assistance is arbitrarily close to $K$. Combined with an upper bound from prior work, this establishes $K$ as the extremal value of the multiplicative gain from NS-assistance across all $K$-sender MAC settings.

</details>


### [272] [New lower bounds for constant-weight codes via seeded bit-swap tabu search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13906)
*William Echols*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文使用带种子初始化的比特交换禁忌搜索算法，发现了124个改进二进制常量权码下界的新构造，并通过增强A(n,8,8)的界，改进了亲吻数τ_32、τ_33、τ_34和τ_37的下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二进制常量权码是编码理论与组合设计中的重要研究对象，确定最大码字数量A(n,d,w)的下界具有理论价值。现有下界有待改进，且改进A(n,8,8)的界可间接改进相关维数下亲吻数的下界。

Method: 采用带种子初始化的比特交换禁忌搜索（seeded initialization with bit-swap tabu search）算法，系统地搜索并构建新的二进制常量权码构造，以改进现有的A(n,d,w)下界。

Result: 发现了124个改进现有二进制常量权码下界的新构造；同时，通过增强A(n,8,8)在n∈{32,33,34,37}处的界，改进了亲吻数τ_32、τ_33、τ_34和τ_37的下界。

Conclusion: 本文提出的搜索算法在改进二进制常量权码下界方面效果显著，且此类改进对亲吻数等紧密相关的编码理论问题具有重要的推论价值。

Abstract: A binary constant-weight code is a set of binary words of length $n$ such that each word has exactly weight $w$ and is at least Hamming distance $d$ from every other word in the set. $A(n,d,w)$ denotes the maximum size of a binary constant-weight code with parameters $(n,d,w)$. Using seeded initialization with bit-swap tabu search, we found 124 new constructions that improve existing lower bounds for $A(n,d,w)$. As a corollary of stronger bounds on $A(n,8,8)$ for $n \in \{ 32,33,34,37 \}$, we also improve lower bounds on kissing numbers $τ_{32}$, $τ_{33}$, $τ_{34}$, and $τ_{37}$.

</details>


### [273] [A Structural Characterization of Entropy Functionals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13917)
*Daniel Lazarev*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文引入一个测度论框架，通过广义均值复合下的容许性条件（熵的有界性要求），完整刻画了所有满足条件的熵泛函，建立了一个由均值生成器、熵标度和可加性假设逐级决定的四层层级结构，并解决了Rényi于1961年提出的关于广义均值能否替代算术均值的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Shannon、Rényi、Tsallis等熵的选择往往是基于惯例而非结构原则；Rényi在1961年提出的问题——哪些广义均值可以替代其熵公理化中的算术均值——一直悬而未决。本文试图建立一个统一的测度论框架，从结构原则出发刻画所有"可容许"的熵泛函及其散度。

Method: 在测度论框架下，定义容许性条件为：当输入测度关于参考测度绝对连续时，输入熵不超过参考熵。在广义均值复合（generalized mean-value composition）条件下，通过生成函数g的凸性/凹性条件（t↦g(1/t)的严格凸性/凹性）来表征容许性，并将该判据等价转化为Csiszár f-散度生成函数的严格凸性。在此基础上引入熵标度和可加性假设（乘积可加性与内部可加性）逐级刻画层级结构。

Result: 完整表征了满足容许性的熵泛函，获得四层层级：连续严格单调生成器g容许当且仅当t↦g(1/t)在g递增时为严格凸、在g递减时为严格凹。该判据等价于关联Csiszár f-散度生成函数的严格凸性，从而在马尔可夫核下具有精确等号条件的数据处理不等式。乘积可加性唯一确定Rényi族，内部可加性（或乘积可加性加上算术均值复合）唯一确定Shannon熵。构造性方法还产生了新的容许熵和散度族，包括积分变换示例。

Conclusion: 本文为熵与散度的选择提供了结构性的数学基础，解决了Rényi的开放问题，建立了一个统一的四层层级体系，并揭示了熵容许性、f-散度凸性与数据处理不等式之间的深层联系，同时为构造新的熵与散度族提供了系统方法。

Abstract: Entropy functionals and their associated divergences underlie many statistical methods, including maximum entropy inference, minimum divergence estimation, and goodness-of-fit testing, yet choosing among Shannon, Rényi, Tsallis, and more general entropies is often a matter of convention rather than structural principle. We introduce a measure theoretic framework in which admissibility requires the entropy of an input measure to be bounded above by that of its reference measure whenever the former is absolutely continuous with respect to the latter. Under generalized mean-value composition, we characterize all such entropy functionals and obtain a four-level hierarchy determined successively by the mean generator, entropy scale, and additivity assumptions. A continuous strictly monotone generator $g$ is admissible exactly when $t\mapsto g(1/t)$ is strictly convex for increasing $g$, or strictly concave for decreasing $g$. This resolves a question posed by Rényi (Proc. 4th Berkeley Sympos. Math. Statist. Prob., 1961) concerning which generalized means may replace the arithmetic mean in his entropy axiomatization. The same criterion is equivalent to strict convexity of an associated Csiszár $f$-divergence generator and therefore yields data processing under Markov kernels with an exact equality condition. Within this hierarchy, product additivity singles out the Rényi family, while internal additivity, or product additivity together with arithmetic mean-value composition, singles out Shannon entropy. The characterization is constructive and yields new admissible entropy and divergence families, including integral-transform examples.

</details>


### [274] [Integrated Information in the Active Inference Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14165)
*Carlotta Langer,Jesse van Oostrum,Nihat Ay*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文结合主动推理框架与整合信息理论，通过在生成模型上引入结构假设定义整合信息度量，实验发现整合信息度量与主动推理代理的自由能之间存在随生成模型规模增大而增强的相关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主动推理框架为建模感性行为提供了统一原则，而整合信息理论旨在量化系统的意识水平。将两者结合，可以从整合信息的角度分析主动推理代理，从而更深入地理解智能体行为与意识水平之间的关系。

Method: 将主动推理框架与整合信息理论相结合，针对生成模型引入额外的结构假设，定义了一个整合信息度量，并通过模拟代理实验验证该度量与主动推理中自由能的关系。

Result: 模拟实验表明，整合信息度量与主动推理代理的自由能之间存在相关性，且该相关性随生成模型规模的增大而增强。

Conclusion: 主动推理框架与整合信息理论的结合是可行的，整合信息度量可作为分析主动推理代理意识水平的新工具，其与自由能的相关性为理解智能体内部动态提供了新视角。

Abstract: The active inference framework provides a principled approach to modeling sentient behavior. In this framework perception and action selection are treated in a unified way. The resulting agents form an internal generative model of the relevant dynamics of the world in order to infer their future observations, their internal states and to select actions. We combine this modeling framework with the Integrated Information Theory of consciousness and are therefore able to analyze the active inference agents from the perspective of integrated information. The Integrated Information Theory aims at quantifying the level of consciousness of a system by assessing its capability to integrate information. Here, we define a measure of integrated information for the generative model by making an additional structural assumption. Experiments with simulated agents reveal a correlation between integrated information measures and the free energy of the active inference agents that increases with the size of the generative model.

</details>


### [275] [Positioning with Flexible Reflectors: Solution and Performance Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14184)
*Jiajun He,Han Yu,Danyan Lin,Gaofeng Pan,Hing Cheung So,Stefano Buzzi,Hien Quoc Ngo*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究了柔性反射面（FR）辅助的目标定位问题，在视距路径不可用的情况下，利用FR创建的虚拟视距路径实现目标的简单且精确的定位估计，并推导了克拉美罗下界（CRLB）用于优化FR的位置和方向，同时分析了随机部署下CRLB的分布特性，为FR部署和网络设计提供指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在严重的遮挡（blockage）场景下，传统视距（LoS）路径不可用，导致目标定位困难。柔性反射面（FRs）作为一种低成本、高能效的解决方案，可以通过重塑电磁传播环境来创建虚拟视距路径，从而支持无LoS条件下的目标定位，但目前相关研究尚不充分。

Method: 1) 提出一种利用FR群（FR-swarm）创建的虚拟LoS路径进行目标定位的简单准确估计器；2) 推导CRLB以表征所提出方案的性能极限；3) 利用CRLB优化FR的位置和方向（位置与朝向）；4) 考虑FR随机部署场景，刻画CRLB的分布特性，分析不同网络配置对定位精度的影响。

Result: 仿真结果表明，所提出的定位方案性能接近CRLB理论下界；推导的解析结果能够为FR部署和网络设计提供有效指导。

Conclusion: FR群辅助定位方案在无LoS条件下能够实现接近CRLB的定位性能，CRLB分析可用于优化FR部署参数，随机部署场景下的CRLB分布分析揭示了网络配置对定位精度的影响规律，为实际FR网络部署提供了理论依据。

Abstract: Flexible reflectors (FRs) have emerged as a low-cost and energy-efficient solution for reshaping electromagnetic propagation environments across a wide range of applications. This paper investigates FR-swarm-assisted target localization in scenarios where line-of-sight (LoS) paths are unavailable. By leveraging the virtual LoS paths created by the FRs, a simple yet accurate estimator is proposed for localization under severe blockage conditions. To characterize the performance limits of the proposed scheme, we derive the Cramer-Rao lower bound (CRLB) and use it to optimize the positions and orientations of the FRs. Furthermore, by accounting for random FR deployment, we characterize the CRLB distribution and reveal how different network configurations affect localization accuracy. Simulation results demonstrate that the developed scheme closely approaches the CRLB performance, while the derived analytical results provide useful guidelines for FR deployment and network design.

</details>


### [276] [Lossy Compression via Sparse Regression Codes: Generalized Construction and Finite-length Bounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14494)
*Galen Reeves,Ramji Venkataramanan*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究了用于有损压缩的稀疏回归码（SPARCs）在简单贪心编码规则下的性能，推广了SPARC构造到"加法正交回归码"类别，推导了非渐近的平方误差失真界，并基于功率分配优化提升了有限长度压缩性能和下界复杂度变体的失真正式保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SPARCs虽然在有损压缩中展现出潜力，但其编码规则和构造的推广性尚未被充分探索；现有的功率分配策略缺乏系统性优化，且对于低复杂度变体（如符号SPARCs和K-稀疏SPARCs）缺乏严格的失真保证。作者希望通过统一的框架分析贪心编码下的失真演化，并提供可优化的理论指导。

Method: 将标准SPARC推广为"加法正交回归码"（additive orthogonal regression codes）这一更广泛的类别；考虑基于相关性和基于距离的两种贪心编码规则；通过追踪编码残差在各级（stages）之间的演化，推导非渐近的平方误差失真界；基于所得界分析功率分配对失真的影响，并据此优化功率分配方案。

Result: 所推导的非渐近失真界揭示了功率分配在控制失真中的关键作用；通过优化功率分配，有限长度下的压缩性能得到提升；该框架同时为符号SPARCs和K-稀疏SPARCs等低复杂度变体提供了失真保证。

Conclusion: 贪婪编码规则下的类SPARC码可以通过合理的功率分配实现性能优化，加法正交回归码框架为理解和改进SPARC系编码方案的有限长度性能提供了统一的理论工具。

Abstract: We study sparse regression codes (SPARCs) for lossy compression under simple greedy encoding rules, including both correlation-based and distance-based methods. We generalize the SPARC construction, and consider the class of \emph{additive orthogonal} regression codes, of which standard SPARCs are a special case. For this class of codes, we derive nonasymptotic bounds on the squared-error distortion by tracking the evolution of the encoding residual across stages. Our results highlight the role of power allocation in controlling the distortion, allowing us to optimize the allocation based on the parameters of the code. The optimized allocation improves the finite-length compression performance of SPARCs, and our bounds provide distortion guarantees for lower complexity variants of SPARCs, like signed SPARCs and $K$-sparse SPARCs.

</details>


### [277] [Learning-to-Transition for Large-scale and High-Order MIMO Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14511)
*Yubo Zhang,Yiyao Liu,Xiaodong Wang*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出了学习到转移（L2T）框架，将MIMO检测建模为完整向量转移的随机序列，采用信道耦合Transformer更新嵌入与采样策略，通过分块自回归分解捕捉流间依赖。硬输出通过残差到BER课程训练，软输出通过将硬策略参数级克隆至未绑定的迭代检测解码接收机实现，并采用多阶段训练策略稳定硬到软的迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高阶MIMO检测需要在巨大的离散符号空间中高效搜索，同时为信道解码生成可靠的软信息，这是一个极具挑战性的组合优化问题。

Method: (1) 将MIMO检测重构为随机完整向量转移序列；每步转移中，信道耦合Transformer同时更新实例嵌入和采样策略；(2) 采用分块自回归分解以适度的顺序复杂度刻画流间依赖；(3) 硬输出检测通过残差到BER课程训练，先学习精确残差度量的搜索几何，再与传输比特精度对齐；(4) 软输出通过参数级克隆将硬策略转移至未绑定SISO IDD接收机的每一层，实现捆绑到未捆绑的迁移；(5) 在每个IDD轮次中，解码器先验根据贝叶斯规则倾斜候选生成，似然加权的终态假设产生后验和外在对数似然比用于LDPC解码；(6) 多阶段训练策略逐步暴露合成与循环解码器先验来稳定迁移。

Result: 该框架证明了硬-软策略迁移的有效性，通过参数级克隆保留零先验搜索动力学，同时允许在解码器反馈下实现逐层和逐轮的专门化，多阶段训练进一步稳定了从硬输出到软输出的迁移过程。

Conclusion: L2T框架为MIMO检测提供了一种统一的学习驱动方案，有效桥接了硬输出与软输出检测，通过课程学习和策略迁移实现高效搜索与可靠软信息生成的兼顾。

Abstract: High-order multiple-input multiple-output (MIMO) detection requires efficient search over a large discrete symbol space while producing reliable soft information for channel decoding. This paper develops a learning-to-transition (L2T) framework that formulates MIMO detection as a stochastic sequence of complete-vector transitions. At each transition, a channel-coupled Transformer updates both the instance embedding and the sampling policy, while a blockwise autoregressive factorization captures inter-stream dependence with moderate sequential complexity. For hard-output detection, a transition network is applied recursively and trained through a residual-to-BER curriculum, which first learns the MIMO search geometry from the exact residual metric and then aligns the policy with transmitted-bit accuracy. For soft-output reception, the well-trained hard policy is cloned at the parameter level into every layer of an untied soft-input soft-output iterative detection and decoding (IDD) receiver. This tied-to-untied transfer preserves the learned zero-prior search dynamics while enabling layer- and round-specific specialization under decoder feedback. Within each IDD round, decoder priors tilt candidate generation according to Bayes' rule, and likelihood-weighted terminal hypotheses produce posterior and extrinsic log-likelihood ratios for LDPC decoding. A multi-stage training strategy further stabilizes the hard-to-soft transfer by progressively exposing the receiver to synthetic and in-loop decoder-generated priors.

</details>


<div id='cs.GR'></div>

# cs.GR [[Back]](#toc)

### [278] [Strand-based Hairstyle Generation via Large Reconstruction and Multimodal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13679)
*Conghui Hao,Tao Huang,Yuefan Shen,Tongtong Wang,Zhongtian Zheng,Kui Wu*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需任务特定训练数据的自动管线，结合Large Reconstruction Models、Large Multimodal Models和经典几何处理，从单视角图像生成高质量、可直接用于生产的发丝级（strand-based）发型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前生产管线中创建高质量发丝级发型高度依赖熟练艺术家和耗时的手动创作，成本高且难以规模化。现有的基于学习方法虽然推进了图像驱动的发型重建，但通常需要大量多样的训练数据，难以泛化到复杂发型（如发髻、马尾），并且输出表示与发丝级建模、编辑和仿真不直接兼容。

Method: 提出一种自动管线，结合三类技术：(1) Large Reconstruction Models (LRMs) 用于从单视角图像生成粗粒度几何；(2) Large Multimodal Models (LMMs) 提供语义理解和分层指导；(3) 经典几何处理方法对生成结果进行细化和优化，获得发丝级的高质量几何。整个流程无需任务特定训练或数据收集。

Result: 在多种发型上（直发、卷发、短发、长发以及马尾和发髻等复杂的结构化造型）均能生成视觉效果出色的发丝级重建结果，且整个流程只需数分钟即可完成，适合集成到现代数字人工作流中。

Conclusion: 该方法在保持发丝级表示兼容性的同时，解决了现有方法对大规模训练数据依赖和复杂发型泛化能力不足的问题，提供了快速、自动、生产级质量的单图发型重建方案。

Abstract: Creating high-quality strand-based hairstyles in current production pipelines remains heavily dependent on skilled artists and time-consuming manual authoring, making it costly and difficult to scale. Existing learning-based methods have advanced image-driven hair reconstruction, but typically require large, diverse training datasets, struggle to generalize to complex styles such as buns and ponytails, and often operate in representations that are not directly compatible with strand-based modeling, editing, and simulation. We present a novel automatic pipeline that combines the capabilities of Large Reconstruction Models (LRMs), Large Multimodal Models (LMMs), and classical geometry processing to generate high-quality strand-based hairstyles from single-view images. Our approach produces detailed, production-ready strand geometry without task-specific training or data collection and can handle a wide variety of hairstyles, including straight and curly hair, short and long styles, and challenging structured configurations such as ponytails and buns. Across this diverse set of examples, our method generates visually compelling strand-level reconstructions within only a few minutes, making it well-suited for integration into modern digital human workflows.

</details>


### [279] [Uncertainty-Aware Jacobi Set Computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14409)
*Daniel Klötzl,Daniel Weiskopf*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种考虑不确定性的Jacobi集计算方法，基于多元正态分布模型对成对标量场的不确定性进行解析传播，将原有基于边的Jacobi集识别方法扩展到不确定输入，并提供专门设计的可视化映射展示Jacobi集及不确定性的多个侧面。方法在解析数据集上通过蒙特卡洛验证，并应用于流体动力学和天气集成两个实际案例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Jacobi集是用于多场可视化的重要拓扑描述子，用于捕捉两个标量场的梯度对齐情况。然而，现有的Jacobi集计算方法假设输入是确定的，无法处理实际数据中普遍存在的不确定性（如测量误差、模拟噪声等）。因此需要一种能够感知不确定性、并在不确定性条件下仍能可靠计算Jacobi集的方法。

Method: 首先采用并重新表述现有的基于三角剖分上边识别（edge-based identification）的Jacobi集计算框架；然后基于多元正态分布构建一个空间连贯的成对标量场不确定性模型；通过解析方式传播不确定性，将Jacobi集计算推广到不确定输入；最后设计可视化映射与叠加层，展示Jacobi集及不同维度的不确定性信息。

Result: 在解析数据集上将所提不确定性模型和不确定性感知方法与蒙特卡洛方法进行对比验证，证实了方法的有效性；随后在流体动力学和天气集成两个实际用例中成功应用。

Conclusion: 该工作成功将Jacobi集拓扑描述子的计算从确定输入扩展到不确定输入场景，通过解析不确定性传播替代昂贵的蒙特卡洛采样，并配套了定制的可视化方案，为多场数据的不确定性可视化提供了新途径。

Abstract: We present an uncertainty-aware Jacobi set computation method. In general, Jacobi sets are topological descriptors that capture the gradient alignments of two scalar fields, as, e.g., used for multi-field visualization. We adopt and reformulate an existing computational approach that relies on an edge-based identification of Jacobi set edges on a given triangulation. Our extension to uncertainty visualization builds upon a versatile, spatially coherent uncertainty model for pairs of scalar fields based on multivariate normal distributions. We propagate the uncertainty analytically, thereby lifting the original Jacobi set computation to uncertain inputs. Furthermore, we present an overlay of visual mappings specifically designed to show the Jacobi sets along with different facets of uncertainty information. Both the uncertainty model and uncertainty-aware method are validated against a Monte Carlo approach on an analytic dataset and applied to two use cases from fluid dynamics and weather ensembles.

</details>
